+/// Exhaustion is observable only when the authored read rule itself contains
+/// a top-level absence check on an exact required point keyed by this row's
+/// intrinsic id. The lane stays private and evaluates the complete rule.
+fn vacancy_branch(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane) -> bool {
+    if lane.source.is_some() || lane.scan.filter.is_some() || !lane.scan.prefix.is_empty() { return false; }
+    let Some(outer) = schema.table(&lane.scan.table) else { return false };
+    if outer.sync.is_some() { return false; }
+    outer.rules.read.disjuncts().iter().any(|rule| {
+        let RuleExpr::Not(inner) = rule else { return false };
+        let RuleExpr::Exists { table, index, key, equalities, .. } = inner.as_ref() else { return false };
+        let Some(target) = schema.table(table) else { return false };
+        let Some(point) = target.index(index) else { return false };
+        equalities.is_empty() && key == &[RuleTerm::Field("id".into())]
+            && point.unique && point.is_plain() && point.components.len() == 1
+            && point.components[0].column().is_some_and(|column|
+                target.columns.get(column).is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()))
+    })
+}
+
 /// An intrinsic-id membership lane retains id as its logical merge position
 /// even though the physical byId scan pins that sole component.

@@ -753,7 +773,40 @@ fn membership_witness(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane, null_distinc
             || !equalities.iter().all(|(column, term)| matches(column, term)) { return false; }
         !null_distinct || probe_index.components.iter().all(|part| part.column().is_some_and(|column| column != source_column
             && fixed(column).is_some_and(|expr| matches!(expr, Expr::Input(Input::Viewer)) || fixed_non_null_literal(expr))))
-    }, &|rule| rule_true(rule, &lane.scan.table, outer_table, &pinned))
+    }, &|rule| rule_true(rule, &lane.scan.table, outer_table, &pinned)
+        || lane.scan.filter.as_deref().is_some_and(|filter| fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, filter, rule, &pins)))
+}
+
+/// A generated membership lane may retain a second, prefix-fixed point grant
+/// as a residual predicate.  Recognize only the exact bound point expression:
+/// this proves that every row emitted by the lane satisfies that conjunct,
+/// while a missing, reordered or merely similar probe keeps the merge out.
+// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — one fixed grant may guard one varying per-row grant.
+fn fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema: &Schema, filter: &Expr, rule: &RuleExpr,
+    pins: &[(&String, &Expr)]) -> bool {
+    if let Expr::Binary { op: crate::ir::BinaryOp::And, left, right } = filter {
+        return fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, left, rule, pins)
+            || fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, right, rule, pins);
+    }
+    let (Expr::Exists(read), RuleExpr::Exists { table, index, key, equalities, .. }) = (filter, rule) else { return false };
+    let Some(target) = schema.table(table) else { return false };
+    let Some(point) = target.index(index) else { return false };
+    equalities.is_empty() && target.through.is_none() && target.not_before.is_none() && !target.is_expiring()
+        && point.unique && point.is_plain() && point.components.iter().all(|part| part.column()
+            .and_then(|column| target.columns.get(column)).is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()))
+        && schema.rule_input_is_readable(table, index, key)
+        && &read.table == table && &read.index == index && read.key.len() == key.len()
+        && read.key.iter().zip(key).all(|(expression, term)| rule_term_expression(term, expression, pins))
+}
+
+fn rule_term_expression(term: &RuleTerm, expression: &Expr, pins: &[(&String, &Expr)]) -> bool {
+    match (term, expression) {
+        (RuleTerm::Viewer, Expr::Input(Input::Viewer)) => true,
+        (RuleTerm::Literal(value), Expr::Literal(actual)) => value == actual,
+        (RuleTerm::Field(field), expression) => pins.iter().find_map(|(name, value)|
+            (name.as_str() == field).then_some(*value)).is_some_and(|value| value == expression),
+        _ => false,
+    }
 }
 
 /// A witness supplies one conjunct; every other conjunct must already hold

+/// A top-level `not exists point[.id]` explicitly makes vacancy observable.
+/// One server lane checks the original rule for every candidate and may report
+/// exhaustion only after the physical range ends. It carries no ordinary
+/// filter because rule probes use wider system visibility.
+// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — exact vacancy is the sole non-uniform exhaustion lane.
+pub(super) fn vacancy(tables: &Tables, synthesized: &BTreeSet<(String, String)>, requests: &mut Requests,
+    read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<MergeExpr> {
+    let range = read.range.as_ref()?;
+    let table = tables.get(&read.table)?;
+    let rule = branches::read_rule(table)?;
+    if read.filter.is_some() || read.index.is_some() || !read.keys.is_empty() || read.matching.is_some()
+        || !read.computed.is_empty() || read.cursor_prefix.is_some()
+        || (table.decl.sync.is_some() && !table.decl.online_only) { return None; }
+    fn alternatives<'a>(value: &'a Expr, out: &mut Vec<&'a Expr>) {
+        match value {
+            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" => { alternatives(left, out); alternatives(right, out); }
+            other => out.push(other),
+        }
+    }
+    let mut disjuncts = Vec::new(); alternatives(rule, &mut disjuncts);
+    let admitted = disjuncts.iter().any(|value| {
+        let Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } = value else { return false };
+        let Expr::Exists { read: probe, .. } = value.as_ref() else { return false };
+        if op != "not" || probe.range.is_some() || probe.filter.is_some() || probe.matching.is_some()
+            || !probe.seeks.is_empty() || probe.keys.len() != 1 || intent::column(&probe.keys[0].value) != Some("id") { return false; }
+        let Some(target) = tables.get(&probe.table) else { return false };
+        let Some(columns) = super::intent_lanes::probe_columns(tables, synthesized, table, probe) else { return false };
+        target.indexes.iter().any(|index| target.total_unique(index) && index.columns == columns)
+    });
+    if !admitted { return None; }
+    let mut candidate = requests.clone(); let errors = candidate.error_count();
+    let mut system = read.clone(); candidate.read(tables, &mut system, false);
+    let mut lane = read.clone(); candidate.plan_lane(tables, &mut lane);
+    let mut lanes = Vec::with_capacity(2); let mut order = None;
+    branches::push_membership_lane(&candidate, table, &mut lanes, &mut order, system, SYSTEM_LANE, None, None, None)?;
+    branches::push_membership_lane(&candidate, table, &mut lanes, &mut order, lane, VACANCY_LANE, None, None, None)?;
+    if candidate.error_count() != errors { return None; }
+    *requests = candidate;
+    Some(MergeExpr { view_order: Vec::new(), table: read.table.clone(), matching: None, filter: None,
+        index: String::new(), order: range.order, take: range.take, cursor: range.cursor.clone(), lanes, site: read.site.clone() })
+}

   897	Wave 10 showed that §14's cap requirement excluded the ordinary membership

   953	3. the fixed pins decide the source read rule true, and all existing index,
   954	   key, label, descriptor, total-predicate and shared-position checks pass.
   955	
   956	A nullable original unique is not a total point proof. It can still use PAGE
   957	when discovery pins every column of a separate total unique, or an applicable
   958	cap bounds its discovery group. For example, nullable `unique parent, principal`
   959	remains the positional rule probe while required `unique principal` bounds the
   960	inverted `principal = viewer` discovery to one row. This does not rewrite the
   961	original probe or its null semantics: a null in that selected point retains the
   962	ordinary result of no witness. If a cap is the only missing condition,
   963	`N_NEVER_COMPLETE` gives the exact declaration to add, using the source's
   964	fixed non-null columns: for the usual shape, `cap 50 by principal`; with no
   965	fixed group, `cap 50`. The note also says that a cap is a stored write
   966	constraint: adding it can refuse existing adoption or later inserts once that
   967	group holds 50 live rows (and physically retained rows where cap validation
   968	requires them), so the author should add it only when it is a real product
   969	limit. The note is withheld when a cap cannot repair the
   970	shape, including multiple row-dependent components, a hidden source, a
   971	non-exact or ambiguous probe, a multi-hop target, incompatible ordering, or a
   972	physical key bound. It must not prescribe code that still cannot plan.
   973	
   974	The varying target column may now be the target row's own `id`:
   975	
   976	```quarry
   977	read <- .owner = viewer or exists shares[document = .id, principal = viewer]
   978	```
   979	
   980	Discovery yields document ids, and each dynamic target lane pins `id`. A
   981	one-row id range is trivially ordered, but it still reports the page's shared
   982	logical position, for example `.at, id`: the row supplies `.at`, and `id` is
   983	the final tie-breaker just as in every other lane. All lanes therefore share
   984	the existing version-4/5 cursor and frontier rules. Before any row is emitted,
   985	the engine opens every discovered id lane and reads its one possible head. If
   986	an `examined`, `index_entries`, `rule_probes` or `steps` cut leaves even one
   987	unfinished lane without a head/`through`, §6's frontier is absent and the page
   988	is empty, incomplete and has no continuation: no returned row can sort ahead
   989	of an unread point. Only
   990	after every point lane is exhausted or has a head can the heap emit the true
   991	`.at, id` prefix. Heap exhaustion remains `E_BOUND` as above. The implicit
   992	`byId` is a valid exact source point too, which admits the parent-owner form
   993	
   994	```quarry
   995	read <- .owner = viewer or exists offers[id = .offer, baker = viewer]
   996	```
   997	
   998	when `baker = viewer` decides the offer source readable. Its inverted source
   999	scan uses or synthesizes the plain `baker, id` discovery order (within the
  1000	existing width, byte, label and ambiguity checks), discovers the caller's
  1001	offer ids in the same transaction snapshot, and target lanes pin `.offer`; it
  1002	does not require `cap N by baker`. Both forms preserve the rule's exact
  1003	witness, snapshot, expiry, device-coverage and meter requirements.
  1004	
  1005	Discovery and target scans use their ordinary lifetime filter. A source row
  1006	before `not before` is not a witness and opens no lane; a future target row is
  1007	not a lane head and cannot be emitted early. PAGE contributes no lifetime
  1008	bypass or separate clock. Source and target opening commits invalidate the
  1009	ordinary watched table revisions; expiry joins the existing minimum deadline.
  1010	No opening timestamp is exported in the cursor or `nextExpiry`.
  1011	
  1012	The schema requires `branch-membership-v2` when a membership operation uses a
  1013	metered discovery source or pins the target row's intrinsic `id`. The optional
  1014	operation fact preserves that classification across schema serialization;
  1015	the existing `branch-membership-v1` remains required for every membership
  1016	merge, so capped §14 plans remain v1-compatible. A v1-only engine or device
  1017	refuses a PAGE2 backend by the ordinary capability handshake instead of
  1018	applying its older admission validator or downgrading the plan to an eager
  1019	merge.
  1020	
  1021	The Lean classifier admits row-`id` targets and the same three source proofs:
  1022	a total original point probe, fully pinned total-unique discovery, or an
  1023	applicable cap. The fully pinned proof is checked on the discovery pins and
  1024	does not replace a nullable original positional probe. Its logical `ownerMembership_sound` and
  1025	`ownerMembership_cover` theorems remain about the exact rule leaves; executable
  1026	examples add uncapped composite membership, row-id membership and parent-owner
  1027	pages. Native, Lean and device differential generators draw the same forms,
  1028	including source populations that cross the examined meter and must return the
  1029	empty incomplete discovery result, and id populations whose target-head phase
  1030	cuts before every lane opens. The language, simulator and both device stores
  1031	walk each form through one shared cursor and test overlap de-duplication.
  1032

  1071	## 16. PAGE3: finish the ordinary private-page plans
  1072	
  1073	Commissioned 2026-10-13; implemented locally. Muse and fresh Codex both
  1074	READY in round 5 at `65d505289f`; verbatim reviews and frozen brief are
  1075	`llp/reviews/3000.036-page3.r5.{muse,codex,brief}.md`.
  1076	PAGE2 left 18 notes in 11 of the 50 odd wave-10 final schemas: 12
  1077	party/static-grant pages, three compound rules, two hidden parent sources
  1078	and one pure membership. This amendment extends the existing plan rather
  1079	than asking builders to merge lists themselves. It changes no read authority,
  1080	replicated data or runtime resource ceiling.
  1081	
  1082	**Static grants and whole-group reads.** An owner-or-readable-static-grant
  1083	rule uses the existing party/grant merge on synced union tables too. The
  1084	server evaluates the positive readable grant once; its holder reads the
  1085	original whole range, everyone else the party ranges. An explicit `first` or
  1086	`last` is a page bound, not a table cap: a role holder may page an unbounded
  1087	table, under the usual request meter. An implicit whole-group read is expanded
  1088	only if its original fixed prefix has an applicable stored cap or fully
  1089	pinned total unique, at or below the examined ceiling. That original bound
  1090	applies to the union. A cap on owner does not bound a staff's whole table.
  1091	A fully pinned total unique may supply a one-row lane with the implicit id
  1092	position, even when its physical index has no unpinned component.
  1093	
  1094	**Full union devices.** The same descriptor and scope serve server and device
  1095	cursors. On a device a full union target's grant lane reads its held authorized
  1096	rows, with the ordinary acquisition, coverage and meter checks; it does not
  1097	probe a remote grant. That held range covers every other target lane, so the
  1098	others are not opened. Missing acquisition stays unknown. Online-only and
  1099	single-arm narrowed sync retain their existing grant deferral. This amends
  1100	LLP 3000.036.000.000 §5.3's planner restriction, while preserving its holding
  1101	and acquisition invariants.
  1102	
  1103	**Pure membership.** Remove the syntactic party requirement from §15. A rule
  1104	`exists members[.id, viewer]` or `exists members[.parent, viewer]` has a system
  1105	range and exact membership descriptors, no party descriptors. All source
  1106	readability, unique/cap proofs, total filters, index/key bounds, shared
  1107	positions and discovery/head fences remain. If the source discovery is cut,
  1108	no target rows or cursor are emitted. An empty complete discovery means an
  1109	empty complete page. Point uniqueness still does not cap memberships.
  1110	
  1111	**Positive compound rules.** Distribute positive `and`/`or` into at most seven
  1112	alternatives plus the original system range. Each alternative retains all
  1113	of its row equality conditions. It contains either one exact membership
  1114	witness, or row equality pins and positive row-independent guards. A membership
  1115	conjunct supplies precisely its own authority; all remaining conjuncts must
  1116	be true by the target lane's pins. A pinned membership target instead uses
  1117	a readable page guard, without discovering hidden source rows. No negated
  1118	probe, arithmetic, fallible predicate or second varying source component is
  1119	admitted. Candidates are speculative and fall back when the physical plan
  1120	or bound-IR validator cannot prove the whole admitted shape.
  1121	
  1122	A new guarded-range descriptor evaluates `page_guard_decision` on its own
  1123	pins. A true readable guard opens only that range, not the other alternatives;
  1124	a false guard opens none. It does not cover other lanes. The system range
  1125	covers every lane only for the system. Status/party lanes, discovered parent
  1126	lanes and guarded lanes share one ordered id-tied position and deduplicate
  1127	there, as in §§3–6. Thus if `R = ∨i (Pi ∧ Gi ∧ Mi)` and W is the total page
  1128	predicate, each lane returns exactly `W ∧ Pi ∧ Gi ∧ Mi`; their union is W ∧ R.
  1129	Guard facts and membership sources must be readable, as in §15, so neither
  1130	empty results nor completeness reveal hidden witness rows. Read-meter cuts
  1131	and frontier rules are unchanged; heap exhaustion remains a refusal.
  1132	
  1133	A nullable small enum's `field != null` has a finite positive expansion into
  1134	its declared non-null values. This retains null exclusion exactly; ordinary
  1135	nullable inequalities against other values keep their original scan. The
  1136	wave-10 archive with four species therefore gets four enum ranges plus its
  1137	owner, readable reviewer and system ranges. Compound volunteer-open and
  1138	parent-teacher/status rules preserve each status condition when opening lanes.
  1139	
  1140	**Readable static predicates.** A `where` disjunction may repeat a readable
  1141	static point, such as `.gleaner = viewer or exists coordinators[viewer]`.
  1142	Every point-key expression must be an infallible, read-free name or literal
  1143	(including canonical signed numeric literals); arithmetic, assertions, calls
  1144	and member reads keep the original scan and short circuit. The point must be
  1145	a total unique with fixed terms, readable by those pins or
  1146	its identical self-witness rule, without a lifetime or read-through boundary.
  1147	It remains in the original predicate for every caller, including the system.
  1148	This extension belongs to static party/where branch merges only. Membership
  1149	discovery and compound-rule expansion retain their read-free total filter
  1150	requirement, mirrored separately by model cases 3 and 4. The physical point
  1151	can cut even though its logical predicate is total. The
  1152	branch frontier therefore excludes an interrupted filter candidate; it may
  1153	continue only from an earlier fully evaluated candidate, so retry cannot
  1154	skip the interrupted row. A source its holders cannot read keeps its scan.
  1155	
  1156	A discovered/guard-admitted merge range pays its admission reservation once
  1157	on both runtime hosts, including empty ranges. Device acquired-row trust does
  1158	not add a second prefix-rule reservation to an already admitted lane.
  1159	
  1160	**Compatibility and model.** `branch-pages-v2` fences guarded descriptors,
  1161	held-union merge behavior and fully pinned point-lane admission; it is derived
  1162	from bound plans, serialized as an optional operation fact, and older engines
  1163	refuse it through the ordinary handshake. Existing descriptor bytes remain
  1164	unchanged when none of these new lane types is present. Membership-only plans
  1165	retain the existing membership capability rules. The Lean surface model
  1166	removes its party requirement, and models the admitted positive rule ranges.
  1167	Enums refuse the entire model module with the existing explicit `type-enum`
  1168	reason before any program is declared supported. Compound where-branch
  1169	composition, nullable constant pins and physically nonportable/ambiguous
  1170	compound plans have their own named emitter exclusions. Native static
  1171	predicate keys that are arguments or signed expression nodes use the named
  1172	`page-static-key-expression` model boundary until their separate readability
  1173	classifier is extended; they are unsupported, never counted as oracle passes;
  1174	unsupported fragments remain explicitly named rather than accepted by the
  1175	oracle. Its logical leaf soundness/coverage proof includes pure membership.
  1176	
  1177	**Hidden-source boundary and exact repairs.** PAGE3 does not scan private
  1178	parent rows whose owner cannot necessarily read them. No cap/index alone
  1179	repairs that absence proof. Copy the parent's immutable owner onto the child,
  1180	keep that copy and its parent reference immutable, keep the parent non-expiring,
  1181	and retain the reference with `on delete restrict`, and check the copy against the parent in the child's
  1182	insert rule (and in every trusted seed/backfill). Replace the parent-owner
  1183	read-rule leaf with the copied owner comparison. The copy preserves authority
  1184	only while the parent owner is immutable; a mutable owner needs transactional
  1185	propagation or the original rule. Existing data must be backfilled before
  1186	adoption. The diagnostic names the actual table, reference and owner column
  1187	and supplies exact source edits. It never recommends making parents public.
  1188	
  1189	**PAGE3 qualification and review dispositions (2026-10-14).** Round 1
  1190	repaired null-exempt bounds, parent reference/lifetime preservation, outer
  1191	conjunction guards and capability scope; round 2 repaired fallible static keys,
  1192	block-lowered probe fencing and defensive model bounds. Round 3 named static
  1193	argument/signed-key model exclusions, with zero-oracle Unsupported regressions.
  1194	Round 4 separated readable-static model filters from the read-free gates for
  1195	membership/compound plans, with actual native/model data and completeness
  1196	comparisons. Round 5's two independent reviewers marked the same revision
  1197	READY. No unsupported model case is counted as a pass.
  1198	
  1199	All 50 copied odd wave-10 final schemas compile: 18 notes in 11 schemas
  1200	become 2 in one (both hidden parents). Applying the exact checked copy repair
  1201	to a further copied corpus gives 0 in all 50. Per-shape counts, input/output
  1202	hashes and source edits are in `snapback4/reports/20261013-page3/`. Final
  1203	400-case campaigns have 22,277 native and 12,686 device executions without
  1204	disagreements; Lean's 1,108 executions retain only the three existing C2
  1205	pagination occurrences, with no new signature. Lean audits 59 theorems with
  1206	standard axioms only, runs 74 examples and seven fixtures. Focused language,
  1207	model, simulator and both device-store suites pass.
  1208	
  1209	The clean locked Linux web artifact at `f11a5e72cd` is 2,310,393 B raw /
  1210	704,662 B gzip / 532,356 B brotli; budget 705,162 B = measured gzip + 500 B.
  1211	Family/absence audits and wasm/client builds pass. Later edits affect only
  1212	language/model/tests, preserving these device bytes. No push or publication.
  1213	
  1214	## 17. PAGE4: complete multi-audience pages and exact scopes
  1215	
  1216	Commissioned 2026-10-14 for wave-11 fix #11. This amendment extends the
  1217	existing bounded planner. No read authority, replicated audience, runtime
  1218	ceiling or cursor codec changes. Query unions containing a public status
  1219	are separate from union *sync*: a public sync branch remains excluded.
  1220	
  1221	**Mixed positive alternatives.** Positive distribution also applies to a
  1222	simple disjunction mixing party equality, public/status equality, exact
  1223	membership and readable static grants. Each alternative retains every
  1224	conjunct and the complete original query predicate. Equality lanes,
  1225	guarded ranges and exact discovered membership lanes share the existing
  1226	ordered id-tied merge, with duplicate positions emitted once. At most seven
  1227	alternatives plus the system range fit. Speculative source/target indexes
  1228	are retained only when the bound validator accepts the whole plan.
  1229	
  1230	Named plain page indexes normalize to their declared column order only in
  1231	a speculative merge candidate. A declined candidate keeps its original read.
  1232	An exact owner/member rule whose varying component is necessarily pinned
  1233	can use the existing party/guard merge; alternative-only pins do not qualify.
  1234	
  1235	**Exact where witnesses.** A positive whole `where` conjunct identical to a
  1236	whole read-rule disjunct can define an exact narrower audience: for example
  1237	`posts where exists authors[.id, viewer] first 20 by .at after c`. Discover
  1238	that readable witness under §§14–16's total point and complete-discovery
  1239	proofs. Other audiences cannot contribute rows to W. Every other conjunct
  1240	must be read-free and total. The original predicate remains in admission
  1241	validation; the system descriptor retains the authored witness, because
  1242	system bypasses R but never W. Its prefix also binds the fixed target pins
  1243	into the shared cursor scope. A hidden source keeps the original scan.
  1244	
  1245	**Pinned components of exact witnesses.** A positive alternative may pin
  1246	one component of a multi-column rule probe. Substitute only a necessary
  1247	infallible fixed equality, leaving one varying source component; the core
  1248	validator independently checks the original field term against that exact
  1249	target prefix. Example: `.manager = viewer and exists reporting[report =
  1250	.report, manager = .manager]` discovers reporting rows with manager=viewer,
  1251	then their report values. The other party's alternative inverts the other
  1252	component. Extra named point equalities survive. No arithmetic, missing
  1253	pin, second varying component or hidden source is treated as proof.
  1254	
  1255	**Parent snapshots.** Selecting `s.id` or a stored scalar field of a required
  1256	point-read row image is a fixed lexical value, not a new row read. In a
  1257	standalone page's predicate (returned or assigned), the compiler binds those
  1258	selections once before planning. Optional rows, reference hops, private/maintained fields,
  1259	rebindings and joined paths keep the original predicate. A snapshot does
  1260	not prove that a child's copied band/manager equals its parent's field;
  1261	source repairs must say when their extra pins narrow the authored scope.
  1262	
  1263	**Membership word search.** The existing compact ordinary server search may
  1264	derive its exact two-component total membership audience on an online-only
  1265	table too. Provision the reverse readable source index and scoped words
  1266	index in one checked speculative candidate. Synced targets retain their
  1267	same exact audience requirement. The device program and sync holdings do
  1268	not acquire a predicate; empty needles retain the original browse arm.
  1269	Readability, non-expiring source, portable keys, opening meter refusal,
  1270	word-head merge and original cursor binding remain LLP 3000.028 §3.3's.
  1271	
  1272	**Honest incomplete forms.** A fallback note distinguishes an analyzer
  1273	limitation (the current admitted ranges cannot prove this positive shape)
  1274	from a truly partial read (its range/source cannot establish absence under
  1275	its declared authority). It names the exact complete line when one can be
  1276	verified; scope-narrowing pins say that they narrow the answer. A hidden
  1277	parent retains PAGE3's checked copy/backfill repair. No cap/index-only
  1278	suggestion is offered for an unreadable witness, and no copied relationship
  1279	is assumed from a successful parent read alone.
  1280	
  1281	**Readable negative static points and finite enums.** A negated static
  1282	probe is a page guard only when its selected key is total unique, its source
  1283	is readable at that key and has no maintained-state, read lifetime or read-through boundary.
  1284	Its absence is then an observable authorized fact. A hidden negative probe
  1285	still cannot open a complete range. Required enum inequality against a
  1286	literal expands to its exact remaining variants; optional enum inequality
  1287	against a non-null value stays residual. No varying negative source is
  1288	inverted. This completes the renovation selectionsFor page (its unpaged budget aggregation
  1289	retains an analyzer limitation) without
  1290	turning timed privacy into an unsupported absence claim.
  1291	
  1292	A discovered/guard-admitted merge range pays its admission reservation once
  1293	on both runtime hosts, including empty ranges. Device acquired-row trust does
  1294	not add a second prefix-rule reservation to an already admitted lane.
  1295	
  1296	**Compatibility and model.** Existing descriptor bytes and semantics serve
  1297	mixed unions and exact where witnesses. `branch-pages-v3` fences pinned multi-column witnesses, readable negative
  1298	static guarded/covering ranges and the compiler-owned `RequireUncapped`
  1299	primitive before an older validator or decoder can encounter them. A nullable
  1300	row assertion in a query checks the read meter after evaluating its point;
  1301	a cut raises the internal E_BOUND that the query boundary reports as an
  1302	unavailable incomplete answer, instead of inventing the author's absence error.
  1303	Ordinary absent/hidden rows still use the authored error, and mutation
  1304	assertions retain their transactional policy.
  1305	The independent Lean surface classifier must reproduce positive mixed
  1306	ranges, pinned witness substitution and exact where scope. Static snapshot
  1307	binding is an explicit syntax normalization, like where-guard lowering.
  1308	Word search stays a named unsupported model fragment; it must execute zero
  1309	Lean oracle cases, while native/device tests cover its existing words engine.
  1310	The direct-grant soundness theorem remains positive: negative-grant scalar
  1311	absence/aggregation, fallible-filter fallback and descriptor overflow have the
  1312	explicit `page-negative-grant-fallback` model boundary, including negative
  1313	probes nested in conjunctions and made static by prefix pins, and overflow of
  1314	distributed alternatives inside conjunctions, with zero oracle
  1315	executions. Standard bounded negative-grant pages use the independent range
  1316	classifier and are exercised against the model. No unsupported case is counted
  1317	as a pass. Fresh Muse and Codex reviews both mark semantic revision
  1318	`193f94ea9da7a046f4ce6fe76d7b527e1686ef90` READY. See the
  1319	[verbatim paired reviews and provenance](reviews/3000.036-page4.provenance.md).
  1320	Qualification is recorded separately in the PAGE4 workstream report.
  1321	
  1322	Model fidelity: lexical witness pins use an opaque classifier-only parameter
  1323	term, preserving variable identity instead of converting observed argument
  1324	values to literals or viewer proofs. The oracle rejects that term in authored
  1325	rule JSON; executable authority always evaluates the original rule. Named
  1326	plain orders normalize only within the independent unfiltered mixed-merge
  1327	fragment. Filtered/one-party named candidates outside that fragment (including declined
  1328	native candidates) have the explicit
  1329	`page-named-index-fallback` boundary and execute zero oracle cases. No such excluded candidate is claimed to be a native failure. This
  1330	retains native fallback semantics instead of claiming a complete owner pin
  1331	on an authored fixed index. Both review counterexamples have regressions;
  1332	the generator includes argument-valued pinned pairs.
  1333	
  1334	Delivered-history integration: an acquired device history is an audience in
  1335	its own right under LLP 3000.007 §18.2, even after live membership expires.
  1336	A membership merge on such a target selects its existing whole-range system
  1337	descriptor on the device without changing the actual viewer. It retains the
  1338	authored W, acquisition, lifetime, retention, meters and shared cursor scope;
  1339	only current-source rediscovery is bypassed. Server execution is unchanged.
  1340	`branch-pages-v3` fences this device interpretation. Existing expiry/paged
  1341	snapshot history regressions run on both stores. NARR retains ownership of
  1342	P9's general unbounded-sync/empty-partition narration; this compatibility
  1343	correction arose from PAGE4 named-order planning.
  1344	
  1345	## 18. PAGE5: bounded products, fixed guards and exact vacancies
  1346	
  1347	Commissioned 2026-10-14 for wave-12 fix #12. PAGE5 extends the bounded PAGE
  1348	planner only where every admitted lane still has a constructive exhaustion
  1349	argument. It does not turn arbitrary Boolean authorization into a complete
  1350	query plan.
  1351	
  1352	**Bounded products.** The planner distributes independent positive
  1353	alternatives across conjunctions before it classifies the resulting lanes.
  1354	For example, two exact principal alternatives and three exact status
  1355	alternatives produce six lanes, each retaining both selected predicates and
  1356	the original query predicate. Distribution is all-or-nothing and remains
  1357	inside the existing eight-lane limit, including the system lane. Overflow,
  1358	negative alternatives and a residual second varying witness retain the
  1359	analyzer-limitation fallback.
  1360	
  1361	**Fixed grants beside one varying grant.** One dynamic membership witness may
  1362	be accompanied by fixed `exists` guards. A fixed guard is admissible only when
  1363	the lane pins every field term in its target key and the target exposes a
  1364	required, total, unique point that is readable under the target's system
  1365	rule. The target must have no hidden source, read-through boundary,
  1366	`not before` boundary or expiry. The compiler substitutes the exact lane pin
  1367	into the guard and keeps that complete guard in the generated query filter;
  1368	the core validator independently matches it to the original rule expression
  1369	and lane pin. Thus the generated guard observes every fixed grant that could
  1370	authorize the authored rule. A hidden, timed, non-total or extra-equality
  1371	guard remains incomplete. Two varying grant sources also remain outside this
  1372	fragment.
  1373	
  1374	**Exact vacancy lanes.** The server may invert a top-level
  1375	`not exists holds[.id]` alternative when `holds.id` is a required, unique,
  1376	plain point and the paged table is online-only. Its vacancy lane scans the
  1377	same full ordered outer range as the system lane and privately authorizes each
  1378	candidate against the original rule; it publishes completion only after that
  1379	authorized walk is exhausted. A predicate-meter or authorization-walk cut
  1380	therefore produces an incomplete page. Filtered vacancy reads, synchronized
  1381	outer tables, compound or non-`id` keys, non-unique points and any shortcut
  1382	around the ordinary per-row authorization check keep the fallback. The target
  1383	may itself be hidden or timed: the private server walk evaluates the authored
  1384	rule's system probe for each candidate, then exposes only the authorized outer
  1385	row. This is a vacancy predicate over the outer row, not a claim that a private
  1386	target's absence is globally observable.
  1387	
  1388	**Index rewrite note.** When every missing rule field is already fixed by an
  1389	independent exact query equality, the note prints one concrete declaration
  1390	using the actual table and collision-free index name, followed by the complete
  1391	rewritten query using that same index. The binder recompiles that repair in
  1392	tests. When those exact equalities do not establish the rule prefix, the note
  1393	does not offer this rewrite.
  1394	
  1395	**Compatibility and model.** `branch-pages-v4` fences the new validator
  1396	interpretations: fixed residual point guards and exact vacancy lanes. A device
  1397	that only understands `branch-pages-v3` must refuse this IR before decoding or
  1398	executing it; bounded products reuse the already validated positive-branch
  1399	lanes, and existing PAGE4 plans keep their prior capability. The Lean surface
  1400	classifier mirrors the three admissions and the private authorization walk.
  1401	Generated differential cases exercise the new lane families on native,
  1402	simulator and device hosts. Forms outside the fragment remain honest analyzer
  1403	limitations rather than completeness claims.

   160	/// A top-level `not exists point[.id]` explicitly makes vacancy observable.
   161	/// One server lane checks the original rule for every candidate and may report
   162	/// exhaustion only after the physical range ends. It carries no ordinary
   163	/// filter because rule probes use wider system visibility.

   172	        || (table.decl.sync.is_some() && !table.decl.online_only) { return None; }
   173	    fn alternatives<'a>(value: &'a Expr, out: &mut Vec<&'a Expr>) {
   174	        match value {
   175	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" => { alternatives(left, out); alternatives(right, out); }
   176	            other => out.push(other),
   177	        }
   178	    }
   179	    let mut disjuncts = Vec::new(); alternatives(rule, &mut disjuncts);
   180	    let admitted = disjuncts.iter().any(|value| {

   182	        let Expr::Exists { read: probe, .. } = value.as_ref() else { return false };

   186	        let Some(columns) = super::intent_lanes::probe_columns(tables, synthesized, table, probe) else { return false };

   188	    });

   193	    let mut lanes = Vec::with_capacity(2); let mut order = None;
   194	    branches::push_membership_lane(&candidate, table, &mut lanes, &mut order, system, SYSTEM_LANE, None, None, None)?;

   196	    if candidate.error_count() != errors { return None; }

   199	        index: String::new(), order: range.order, take: range.take, cursor: range.cursor.clone(), lanes, site: read.site.clone() })
   200	}

   202	fn compound(rule: &Expr) -> bool {
   203	    match rule {
   204	        Expr::Binary { op, .. } if op == "and" || op == "!=" => true,
   205	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" => compound(left) || compound(right),
   206	        _ => false,
   207	    }
   208	}

   210	/// At most seven positive alternatives plus the system range. A nullable
   211	/// enum's `!= null` is exactly its finite set of non-null values.
   212	fn dnf(table: &TableInfo, value: &Expr) -> Option<Vec<Vec<Expr>>> {
   213	    let out = match value {
   214	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" => {
   215	            let mut out = dnf(table, left)?; out.extend(dnf(table, right)?); out
   216	        }
   217	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "and" => {
   218	            let a = dnf(table, left)?; let b = dnf(table, right)?;
   219	            if a.len().saturating_mul(b.len()) >= branches::MAX_BRANCHES { return None; }
   220	            a.into_iter().flat_map(|a| b.iter().map(move |b| { let mut out = a.clone(); out.extend(b.clone()); out })).collect()
   221	        }
   222	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, site } if op == "!=" => {
   223	            let (column, term) = if let Some(column) = intent::column(left) { (column, right) } else { (intent::column(right)?, left) };
   224	            let kind = table.columns.get(column)?;
   225	            let TypeKind::Enum { variants } = &kind.kind else { return None; };
   226	            let include = |variant: &String| match term.as_ref() {
   227	                Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Null, .. } if kind.optional => Some(true),
   228	                Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Str(excluded), .. } if !kind.optional => Some(variant != excluded),
   229	                _ => None,
   230	            };
   231	            if variants.iter().any(|variant| include(variant).is_none()) { return None; }
   232	            variants.iter().filter(|variant| include(variant).unwrap()).map(|variant| vec![Expr::Binary { op: "=".into(), left: Box::new(Expr::Member {
   233	                base: Box::new(Expr::Focal { site: site.clone() }), name: column.into(), site: site.clone() }),
   234	                right: Box::new(Expr::Literal { value: Literal::Str(variant.clone()), site: site.clone() }), site: site.clone() }]).collect()
   235	        }
   236	        other => vec![vec![other.clone()]],
   237	    };
   238	    (out.len() < branches::MAX_BRANCHES).then_some(out)
   239	}

   631	    let Some(outer) = schema.table(&lane.scan.table) else { return false };
   632	    if outer.sync.is_some() { return false; }
   633	    outer.rules.read.disjuncts().iter().any(|rule| {
   634	        let RuleExpr::Not(inner) = rule else { return false };
   635	        let RuleExpr::Exists { table, index, key, equalities, .. } = inner.as_ref() else { return false };
   636	        let Some(target) = schema.table(table) else { return false };
   637	        let Some(point) = target.index(index) else { return false };
   638	        equalities.is_empty() && key == &[RuleTerm::Field("id".into())]
   639	            && point.unique && point.is_plain() && point.components.len() == 1
   640	            && point.components[0].column().is_some_and(|column|
   641	                target.columns.get(column).is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()))
   642	    })
   643	}
   644	
   645	/// An intrinsic-id membership lane retains id as its logical merge position
   646	/// even though the physical byId scan pins that sole component.
   647	pub fn membership_point_id(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane) -> bool {
   648	    if !lane.membership() || lane.scan.range.is_some() { return false; }
   649	    let Some(table) = schema.table(&lane.scan.table) else { return false };
   650	    let Some(index) = table.index(&lane.scan.index) else { return false };
   651	    let Some(Expr::Field { base, name: source_column }) = lane.distinct.as_ref() else { return false };
   652	    if !matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(binding) if binding == &lane.binding) { return false; }
   653	    lane.scan.prefix.len() == index.components.len()
   654	        && index.components.last().and_then(|part| part.column()).is_some_and(|column| column == "id")
   655	        && membership_outer_column(schema, lane, source_column) == Some("id")
   656	}

   658	/// The source is readable under its fixed pins, and its row is the exact
   659	/// witness for the outer rule branch that the dynamic prefix opens. A cap on
   660	/// the fixed source group is one admission proof; a total point witness is
   661	/// another. The latter bounds each witness, while the shared read meter safely
   662	/// cuts the otherwise-unbounded inverted discovery before any row is emitted.
   663	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#15 — membership discovery proves each parent range without treating point uniqueness as a global membership cap.
   664	fn membership_branch(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane) -> bool {
   665	    membership_witness(schema, lane, false)
   666	}

   668	/// A null discovered value may open a target lane only when the matching
   669	/// point witness identifies its row without that nullable component. A by-id
   670	/// probe with `parent = null` does; a nullable natural unique does not.
   671	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#14 — fixed-id witnesses preserve nullable residual equality without treating nullable natural keys as points.
   672	pub(crate) fn membership_null_distinct(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane) -> bool {
   673	    membership_witness(schema, lane, true)
   674	}

   676	/// Whether PAGE v1's validator would admit this already-valid membership
   677	/// lane. Capability derivation uses the historical predicate exactly, so
   678	/// capped §14 plans remain v1-compatible while every newly admitted shape is
   679	/// fenced by `branch-membership-v2`.
   680	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#15 — PAGE2 capability negotiation preserves the v1 admission boundary.
   681	pub fn membership_v1(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane) -> bool {
   682	    if !lane.membership() { return false; }
   683	    let Some(source) = lane.source.as_ref().and_then(crate::ir::LaneSource::scan) else { return false };
   684	    let Some(Expr::Field { base, name: source_column }) = lane.distinct.as_ref() else { return false };
   685	    if !matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(name) if name == &lane.binding) { return false; }
   686	    let Some(source_table) = schema.table(&source.table) else { return false };
   687	    let Some(source_index) = source_table.index(&source.index) else { return false };
   688	    let next_component = source.prefix.len() < source_index.components.len()
   689	        && source_index.components.get(source.prefix.len()).and_then(|part| part.column()) == Some(source_column);
   690	    let one_row_source = source.prefix.len() == source_index.components.len()
   691	        && source_index.unique && source_index.is_plain()
   692	        && source_index.components.iter().all(|part| part.column().and_then(|column| source_table.columns.get(column))
   693	            .is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()));
   694	    if source.existence || (!next_component && !one_row_source) || !scan_visibility(schema, source).decided_true { return false; }
   695	    let Bound::Literal(take @ 1..) = source.take else { return false };
   696	    let prefix_has = |field: &String| source_index.components.iter().take(source.prefix.len())
   697	        .any(|part| part.column() == Some(field));
   698	    let cap = source_table.constraints.iter().filter_map(|constraint| match constraint {
   699	        crate::schema::Constraint::Cap { by, max } if by.iter().all(prefix_has) && !source_table.cap_exempts_null(by) => Some(*max),
   700	        _ => None,
   701	    }).min();
   702	    if cap.is_none_or(|cap| cap > take && !one_row_source) { return false; }
   703	    membership_outer_column(schema, lane, source_column).is_some_and(|column| column != "id")
   704	}
   705	
   706	fn membership_outer_column<'a>(schema: &'a Schema, lane: &'a crate::ir::MergeLane, source_column: &str) -> Option<&'a str> {
   707	    let table = schema.table(&lane.scan.table)?;
   708	    let index = table.index(&lane.scan.index)?;
   709	    let mut outer = None;
   710	    for (part, key) in index.components.iter().zip(&lane.scan.prefix) {
   711	        let (Some(column), Some(expr)) = (part.column(), key.scalar()) else { return None };
   712	        if matches!(expr, Expr::Field { base, name } if matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(binding) if binding == &lane.binding) && name == source_column)
   713	            && outer.replace(column).is_some() { return None; }
   714	    }
   715	    outer.map(String::as_str)
   716	}
   717	
   718	fn membership_witness(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane, null_distinct: bool) -> bool {
   719	    let Some(source) = lane.source.as_ref().and_then(crate::ir::LaneSource::scan) else { return false };
   720	    let Some(Expr::Field { base, name: source_column }) = lane.distinct.as_ref() else { return false };
   721	    if !matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(name) if name == &lane.binding) { return false; }
   722	    let Some(source_table) = schema.table(&source.table) else { return false };
   723	    let Some(source_index) = source_table.index(&source.index) else { return false };
   724	    let next_component = source.prefix.len() < source_index.components.len()
   725	        && source_index.components.get(source.prefix.len()).and_then(|part| part.column()) == Some(source_column);
   726	    // A fully pinned required unique identifies at most one source row, so
   727	    // any of that row's columns can be the discovered value. The source need
   728	    // not walk the distinct column as a following index component.
   729	    let one_row_source = source.prefix.len() == source_index.components.len()
   730	        && source_index.unique && source_index.is_plain()
   731	        && source_index.components.iter().all(|part| part.column().and_then(|column| source_table.columns.get(column))
   732	            .is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()));
   733	    if source.existence || (!next_component && !one_row_source)
   734	        || !scan_visibility(schema, source).decided_true { return false; }
   735	    // An applicable declaration can compile to a literal source bound. An
   736	    // exact total point may instead use the ordinary read meter: discovery
   737	    // remains fenced until that metered scan completes.
   738	    if !matches!(source.take, Bound::Meter | Bound::Literal(1..)) { return false; }
   739	    let prefix_has = |field: &String| source_index.components.iter().take(source.prefix.len())
   740	        .any(|part| part.column() == Some(field));
   741	    let capped_source_group = source_table.constraints.iter().any(|constraint| match constraint {
   742	        crate::schema::Constraint::Cap { by, max } if by.iter().all(prefix_has) && !source_table.cap_exempts_null(by) => Some(*max),
   743	        _ => None,
   744	    }.is_some());
   745	    let Some(outer_table) = schema.table(&lane.scan.table) else { return false };
   746	    let Some(outer_index) = outer_table.index(&lane.scan.index) else { return false };
   747	    let mut outer_column: Option<&String> = None;
   748	    for (part, key) in outer_index.components.iter().zip(&lane.scan.prefix) {
   749	        let (Some(column), Some(expr)) = (part.column(), key.scalar()) else { return false };
   750	        if matches!(expr, Expr::Field { base, name } if matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(binding) if binding == &lane.binding) && name == source_column)
   751	            && outer_column.replace(column).is_some() { return false; }
   752	    }
   753	    let Some(outer_column) = outer_column else { return false };
   754	    let fixed = |column: &String| source_index.components.iter().zip(&source.prefix)
   755	        .find_map(|(part, key)| (part.column() == Some(column)).then(|| key.scalar()).flatten())
   756	        .or_else(|| source.filter.as_deref().and_then(|filter| required_source_equality(filter, column)));
   757	    let pins = scan_pinned(outer_table, &lane.scan);
   758	    let pinned = |column: &str| pins.iter().find_map(|(name, value)| (name.as_str() == column).then_some(*value));
   759	    membership_rule(&outer_table.rules.read, &|rule| {
   760	        let RuleExpr::Exists { table, index, key, equalities, .. } = rule else { return false };
   761	        let Some(probe_index) = source_table.index(index) else { return false };
   762	        if table != &source.table || !probe_index.unique || !probe_index.is_plain() || probe_index.components.len() != key.len() { return false; }
   763	        let total_point = probe_index.components.iter().all(|part| part.column()
   764	            .and_then(|column| source_table.columns.get(column)).is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()));
   765	        if !one_row_source && !capped_source_group && !total_point { return false; }
   766	        let matches = |column: &String, term: &RuleTerm| if column == source_column { matches!(term, RuleTerm::Field(field) if field == outer_column) }
   767	            else { fixed(column).is_some_and(|expr| match term {
   768	                // @ref LLP 3000.036.001#17 — an exact target pin fixes one component of a multi-column point witness.
   769	                RuleTerm::Field(field) => pinned(field).is_some_and(|pin| pin == expr),
   770	                _ => term_matches_expr(term, expr),
   771	            }) };
   772	        if !probe_index.components.iter().zip(key).all(|(part, term)| part.column().is_some_and(|column| matches(column, term)))
   773	            || !equalities.iter().all(|(column, term)| matches(column, term)) { return false; }
   774	        !null_distinct || probe_index.components.iter().all(|part| part.column().is_some_and(|column| column != source_column
   775	            && fixed(column).is_some_and(|expr| matches!(expr, Expr::Input(Input::Viewer)) || fixed_non_null_literal(expr))))
   776	    }, &|rule| rule_true(rule, &lane.scan.table, outer_table, &pinned)
   777	        || lane.scan.filter.as_deref().is_some_and(|filter| fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, filter, rule, &pins)))
   778	}
   779	
   780	/// A generated membership lane may retain a second, prefix-fixed point grant
   781	/// as a residual predicate.  Recognize only the exact bound point expression:
   782	/// this proves that every row emitted by the lane satisfies that conjunct,
   783	/// while a missing, reordered or merely similar probe keeps the merge out.
   784	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — one fixed grant may guard one varying per-row grant.
   785	fn fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema: &Schema, filter: &Expr, rule: &RuleExpr,
   786	    pins: &[(&String, &Expr)]) -> bool {
   787	    if let Expr::Binary { op: crate::ir::BinaryOp::And, left, right } = filter {
   788	        return fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, left, rule, pins)
   789	            || fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, right, rule, pins);
   790	    }
   791	    let (Expr::Exists(read), RuleExpr::Exists { table, index, key, equalities, .. }) = (filter, rule) else { return false };
   792	    let Some(target) = schema.table(table) else { return false };
   793	    let Some(point) = target.index(index) else { return false };
   794	    equalities.is_empty() && target.through.is_none() && target.not_before.is_none() && !target.is_expiring()
   795	        && point.unique && point.is_plain() && point.components.iter().all(|part| part.column()
   796	            .and_then(|column| target.columns.get(column)).is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()))
   797	        && schema.rule_input_is_readable(table, index, key)
   798	        && &read.table == table && &read.index == index && read.key.len() == key.len()
   799	        && read.key.iter().zip(key).all(|(expression, term)| rule_term_expression(term, expression, pins))
   800	}
   801	
   802	fn rule_term_expression(term: &RuleTerm, expression: &Expr, pins: &[(&String, &Expr)]) -> bool {
   803	    match (term, expression) {
   804	        (RuleTerm::Viewer, Expr::Input(Input::Viewer)) => true,
   805	        (RuleTerm::Literal(value), Expr::Literal(actual)) => value == actual,
   806	        (RuleTerm::Field(field), expression) => pins.iter().find_map(|(name, value)|
   807	            (name.as_str() == field).then_some(*value)).is_some_and(|value| value == expression),
   808	        _ => false,
   809	    }
   810	}
   811	
   812	/// A witness supplies one conjunct; every other conjunct must already hold
   813	/// by the lane's pins. Distribution does not drop a status/role condition.
   814	fn membership_rule(rule: &RuleExpr, witness: &impl Fn(&RuleExpr) -> bool, pinned: &impl Fn(&RuleExpr) -> bool) -> bool {
   815	    match rule {
   816	        RuleExpr::Or(parts) => parts.iter().any(|part| membership_rule(part, witness, pinned)),
   817	        RuleExpr::And(parts) => parts.iter().enumerate().any(|(at, part)|
   818	            membership_rule(part, witness, pinned) && parts.iter().enumerate().all(|(other, rule)| at == other || pinned(rule))),
   819	        _ => witness(rule),
   820	    }
   821	}
   822	
   823	/// A generated membership discovery filter may retain an exact fixed probe
   824	/// component after its capped prefix (`id = 'm1'`, for example). Only a bare
   825	/// equality in the filter's conjunction is a necessary fact about every
   826	/// discovered row; alternatives and other implications remain unsupported.
   827	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#14 — fixed membership witness columns may be proved by exact source residuals.
   828	fn required_source_equality<'a>(filter: &'a Expr, column: &str) -> Option<&'a Expr> {
   829	    match filter {
   830	        Expr::Binary { op: crate::ir::BinaryOp::And, left, right } =>
   831	            required_source_equality(left, column).or_else(|| required_source_equality(right, column)),
   832	        Expr::Binary { op: crate::ir::BinaryOp::Eq, left, right } if source_field(left, column) => Some(right),
   833	        Expr::Binary { op: crate::ir::BinaryOp::Eq, left, right } if source_field(right, column) => Some(left),
   834	        _ => None,
   835	    }
   836	}
   837	
   838	fn source_field(expr: &Expr, column: &str) -> bool {
   839	    matches!(expr, Expr::Field { base, name }
   840	        if name == column && matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(binding) if binding == "$filter"))
   841	}
   842	
   843	fn term_matches_expr(term: &RuleTerm, expr: &Expr) -> bool {
   844	    match (term, expr) { (RuleTerm::Viewer, Expr::Input(Input::Viewer)) => true,
   845	        (RuleTerm::Literal(value), expr) => literal_matches_expr(value, expr), _ => false }
   846	}
   847	
   848	fn literal_matches_expr(value: &Value, expr: &Expr) -> bool {
   849	    match expr {
   850	        Expr::Literal(other) => value == other,
   851	        Expr::Unary { op: crate::ir::UnaryOp::Neg, .. } => matches!(value, Value::Int(expected)
   852	            if expr_int_literal(expr).is_some_and(|actual| actual == i128::from(*expected))),
   853	        _ => false,
   854	    }
   855	}
   856	
   857	fn expr_int_literal(expr: &Expr) -> Option<i128> {
   858	    match expr {
   859	        Expr::Literal(Value::Int(value)) => Some(i128::from(*value)),
   860	        Expr::Unary { op: crate::ir::UnaryOp::Neg, value } => expr_int_literal(value).map(|value| -value),
   861	        _ => None,
   862	    }
   863	}
   864	
   865	fn fixed_non_null_literal(expr: &Expr) -> bool {
   866	    matches!(expr, Expr::Literal(value) if value != &Value::Null) || expr_int_literal(expr).is_some()
   867	}

   105	    /// The page and its continuation candidate (`None` when it ends).
   106	    fn merge_branch_lanes(&mut self, merge: &Merge, prefixes: Vec<Vec<Value>>, take: usize, after: Option<(Vec<Value>, bool)>) -> Result<(Vec<Value>, bool, Option<Vec<Value>>), RuntimeError> {
   107	        let order = merge.lanes[0].scan.order;
   108	        let site = merge.site.as_str();
   109	        let metered_merge = merge.lanes.iter().any(MergeLane::membership);
   110	        // System/grant lanes can cover every other lane. Membership discovery
   111	        // finishes before any target row may be emitted.

   122	        for (lane, prefix) in numbered.iter().filter(|(lane, _)| lane.system())

   137	                        if let Some(slot) = self.env.get_mut(&lane.binding) { *slot = value; }
   138	                        else {
   139	                            self.meter.heap(word_heap::text(&lane.binding) + word_heap::insertion::<String, Value>(self.env.len()))?;
   140	                            self.env.insert(lane.binding.clone(), value);

   143	                        if !self.read_charge(MeterKind::Steps, 1)? { break; }

   145	                        // source-field copies are allocated. The full bound
   146	                        // row is conservative for a prefix that projects it.

   150	                    }
   151	                    Ok(prefixes)

   153	                if let Some(prior) = prior { *self.env.get_mut(&lane.binding).expect("saved binding") = prior; } else { self.env.remove(&lane.binding); }
   154	                let Some(prefixes) = self.membership_discovery_steps(prefixes)? else { return Ok((Vec::new(), false, None)); };
   155	                if self.value_source_incomplete || self.read_capped() { return Ok((Vec::new(), false, None)); }
   156	                for prefix in prefixes {
   157	                    let opened = self.open_branch_lane(lane, prefix, after.as_ref(), Some(Default::default()), true)?;
   158	                    self.push_branch_lane(&mut lanes, opened, true, site)?;
   159	                }

   161	            }
   162	            let definition = &self.schema.tables[&lane.scan.table];
   163	            let index = &definition.indexes[&lane.scan.index];
   164	            let mut grant = None;
   165	            if lane.grant() || lane.guarded() {
   166	                // @ref LLP 3000.036.001#16 — a full union replica walks its
   167	                // held authorized rows; it never decides a remote role probe.
   168	                // scan_consumed still checks acquisition and meters the page.
   169	                let held_union = lane.grant() && self.device && definition.sync.as_ref()
   170	                    .is_some_and(|sync| sync.is_union());
   171	                if !self.context.is_system() && !held_union {
   172	                    let rule = if lane.guarded() { rules::page_guard_decision(self.schema, &lane.scan) }
   173	                        else { rules::covering_grant(self.schema, &lane.scan) };
   174	                    let Some(rule) = rule else { continue };
   175	                    // @ref LLP 3000.036.001#7 — a device decides a seat (`viewer = 'service:x'`), which reads no row;
   176	                    // a probe guard (a role, a pinned parent) it does not decide (LLP 3000.036 §12.1): the read is unknown and the client asks the server.
   177	                    if self.device && rules::rule_probes(&rule) > 0 {
   178	                        self.unknown_fact(&lane.scan.table, &lane.scan.index, prefix, site);
   179	                        continue;
   180	                    }
   181	                    let (allowed, pending) = self.grant_verdict(definition, index, prefix, &rule)?;
   182	                    if !allowed { continue; }

   191	        // A page per lane: k small reads instead of a seek per returned row.
   192	        let batch = take + 1;
   193	        let mut heap = BinaryHeap::new();
   194	        for position in 0..lanes.len() {
   195	            // A lane the meter never reached has examined nothing.
   196	            if self.read_capped() { break; }
   197	            self.fill_branch(&mut lanes[position], batch)?;
   198	            let lane = &mut lanes[position];
   199	            if let Some(row) = lane.buffer.pop_front() {
   200	                self.meter.step(1)?;
   201	                self.charge_branch_position(lane, &row)?;
   202	                let key = self.branch_position(lane, &row);
   203	                Self::push_branch_head(&mut self.meter, &mut heap, MergeHead { key, row, lane: position, order }, metered_merge)?;
   204	            }
   205	        }
   206	        let mut seen = BTreeSet::<String>::new();
   207	        let mut page = Vec::new();
   208	        let mut last = None;
   209	        let mut cut_frontier: Option<Option<usize>> = None;
   210	        while let Some(head) = heap.peek() {
   211	            // @ref LLP 3000.036.001#6 — after a cut, emit only what every lane has examined:
   212	            // the page is a true prefix of the merged order and continues from the frontier.
   213	            if self.read_capped() {
   214	                let frontier = *cut_frontier.get_or_insert_with(|| Self::frontier_index(&lanes, order));
   215	                if !Self::within(&head.key, frontier.and_then(|n| lanes[n].through.as_ref()), order) { break; }
   216	            }
   217	            let head = heap.pop().expect("peeked head");

   219	            let id = head.row.field("id")?.as_str()?;
   220	            if !seen.contains(id) {
   221	                if metered_merge { self.meter.heap(word_heap::text(id) + word_heap::insertion::<String, ()>(seen.len()))?; }
   222	                seen.insert(id.to_owned());
   223	                if page.len() == take { return Ok((page, true, last)); }
   224	                if metered_merge { self.meter.heap(DisclosedKeys(&head.key).heap())?; }
   225	                last = Some(head.key.clone());
   226	                if metered_merge { word_heap::push(&mut self.meter, &mut page, head.row)?; } else { page.push(head.row); }
   227	            }
   228	            let lane = &mut lanes[head.lane];
   229	            lane.bounds.at_or_after = None;
   230	            lane.bounds.after = Some(head.key);
   231	            if lane.buffer.is_empty() && !lane.done && !self.read_capped() { self.fill_branch(lane, batch)?; }
   232	            let lane = &mut lanes[head.lane];
   233	            if let Some(row) = lane.buffer.pop_front() {
   234	                self.charge_branch_position(lane, &row)?;
   235	                let key = self.branch_position(lane, &row);
   236	                Self::push_branch_head(&mut self.meter, &mut heap, MergeHead { key, row, lane: head.lane, order }, metered_merge)?;
   237	            }
   238	        }
   239	        if !self.read_capped() { return Ok((page, false, None)); }
   240	        // Cut: every row up to the frontier is on the page, so the page
   241	        // continues from there; with a lane that examined nothing, it ends.
   242	        let frontier = cut_frontier.unwrap_or_else(|| Self::frontier_index(&lanes, order));
   243	        Ok((page, false, frontier.and_then(|n| lanes[n].through.take())))

   245	
   246	    fn open_branch_lane<'a>(&mut self, lane: &'a MergeLane, prefix: Vec<Value>, after: Option<&(Vec<Value>, bool)>, grant: Option<super::super::expiry::Pending>, metered: bool) -> Result<Lane<'a>, RuntimeError> {
   247	        let scan = &lane.scan;
   248	        let definition = &self.schema.tables[&scan.table]; let index = &definition.indexes[&scan.index];
   249	        // A branch cursor was validated once above. Do not clone the compiled
   250	        // Scan merely to clear it; evaluate only the remaining bounds here.
   251	        let mut bounds = self.word_bounds(scan, definition, index, None, metered, true)?;
   252	        if metered && let Some((values, _)) = after { self.meter.heap(DisclosedKeys(values).heap())?; }
   253	        let point_id = rules::membership_point_id(self.schema, lane);
   254	        let mut done = false;
   255	        if point_id {
   256	            // The point's pinned id is also its logical page position. Decide
   257	            // the shared cursor here instead of applying it after the fully
   258	            // consumed physical prefix.
   259	            if let Some((values, inclusive)) = after {
   260	                let position = &prefix[prefix.len() - 1..];
   261	                let comparison = Self::compare(position, values, scan.order);
   262	                done = comparison == Ordering::Less || comparison == Ordering::Equal && !inclusive;
   263	            }
   264	        } else {
   265	            match after { Some((values, true)) => bounds.at_or_after = Some(values.clone()), Some((values, false)) => bounds.after = Some(values.clone()), None => {} }
   266	        }
   267	        let suffix = index.components.iter().skip(scan.prefix.len()).filter_map(|component| component.key_name());
   268	        let (position_len, ends_in_id) = suffix.fold((0usize, false), |(len, _), name| (len + 1, name == "id"));
   269	        let position_len = position_len + usize::from(!ends_in_id || point_id);
   270	        Ok(Lane { scan, prefix, bounds, buffer: VecDeque::new(), done, grant, vacancy: lane.vacancy(), metered, position_len, through: None })
   271	    }
   272	
   273	    /// Read a lane's next batch strictly after its position, recording how
   274	    /// far it examined.
   275	    fn fill_branch(&mut self, lane: &mut Lane<'_>, batch: usize) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   276	        if lane.done { return Ok(()); }
   277	        // Arm the capture with the lane's own table and index: `scan_consumed`
   278	        // then records the last rule-visible candidate this read examined,
   279	        // filter rejects included (never a nested read's key).
   280	        if lane.metered { self.meter.heap(word_heap::text(&lane.scan.table) + word_heap::text(&lane.scan.index))?; }
   281	        let prior = self.scan_frontier.replace((lane.scan.table.clone(), lane.scan.index.clone(), None));
   282	        let rows = self.read_branch(lane, batch);
   283	        let examined = std::mem::replace(&mut self.scan_frontier, prior).and_then(|(_, _, key)| key);
   284	        let rows = rows?;
   285	        let capped = self.read_capped();
   286	        lane.done = rows.len() < batch && !capped;
   287	        let mut read_to = None;
   288	        if capped && let Some(key) = examined {
   289	            if lane.metered { self.meter.heap(word_heap::key_decode(lane.prefix.len() + lane.position_len, key.len()))?; }
   290	            if let Some(mut values) = crate::key::decode(&key) {
   291	                if lane.prefix.len() <= values.len() { values.drain(..lane.prefix.len()); }
   292	                values.truncate(lane.position_len);
   293	                if values.len() == lane.position_len { read_to = Some(values); }
   294	            }
   295	        }
   296	        if read_to.is_none() && let Some(row) = rows.last() {
   297	            self.charge_branch_position(lane, row)?;
   298	            read_to = Some(self.branch_position(lane, row));
   299	        }
   300	        if read_to.is_some() { lane.through = read_to; }
   301	        lane.buffer = rows.into();
   302	        Ok(())
   303	    }
   304	
   305	    /// The position every unfinished lane has examined through: the least in
   306	    /// the page's order, or `None` when some lane examined nothing past the
   307	    /// cursor. A finished lane bounds nothing.
   308	    fn frontier_index(lanes: &[Lane<'_>], order: Order) -> Option<usize> {
   309	        let mut least: Option<usize> = None;
   310	        for (position, lane) in lanes.iter().enumerate().filter(|(_, lane)| !lane.done) {
   311	            let through = lane.through.as_ref()?;
   312	            if least.is_none_or(|n| Self::compare(through, lanes[n].through.as_ref().expect("frontier"), order) == Ordering::Less) { least = Some(position); }
   313	        }
   314	        least
   315	    }
   316	
   317	    fn compare(a: &[Value], b: &[Value], order: Order) -> Ordering {
   318	        let ordering = a.iter().zip(b).find_map(|(x, y)| match x.total_cmp(y) { Ordering::Equal => None, other => Some(other) }).unwrap_or(a.len().cmp(&b.len()));
   319	        if order == Order::Asc { ordering } else { ordering.reverse() }
   320	    }
   321	
   322	    /// `key` is at or before `through` in the page's order.
   323	    fn within(key: &[Value], through: Option<&Vec<Value>>, order: Order) -> bool {
   324	        through.is_some_and(|through| Self::compare(key, through, order) != Ordering::Greater)
   325	    }
   326	
   327	    /// One read of a lane strictly after its current position. A lane its
   328	    /// grant admitted reads as a decided page (`scan_consumed`'s grant arm).
   329	    fn read_branch(&mut self, lane: &Lane<'_>, batch: usize) -> Result<Vec<Value>, RuntimeError> {
   330	        self.merge_grant = lane.grant;
   331	        self.merge_vacancy = lane.vacancy;
   332	        if lane.metered { self.meter.heap(DisclosedKeys(&lane.prefix).heap() + DisclosedBounds(&lane.bounds).heap())?; }
   333	        let result = self.scan_consumed(&lane.scan, lane.prefix.clone(), lane.bounds.clone(), batch, None, None, false, lane.metered);
   334	        self.merge_grant = None;
   335	        self.merge_vacancy = false;
   336	        Ok(result?.0)
   337	    }
   338	
   339	    fn branch_prefix_values(&mut self, scan: &Scan, metered: bool) -> Result<Vec<Value>, RuntimeError> {
   340	        if metered { self.meter.heap(word_heap::slots::<Value>(scan.prefix.len()))?; }
   341	        let mut values = Vec::with_capacity(scan.prefix.len());
   342	        for key in &scan.prefix {
   343	            values.push(match key {
   344	                IndexKeyExpr::Scalar(expr) => self.expr(expr)?,
   345	                IndexKeyExpr::WordPrefix(_) => return Err(RuntimeError::Schema("a branch prefix is never a words prefix".into())),
   346	            });
   347	        }
   348	        Ok(values)
   349	    }
   350	
   351	    fn clone_branch_prefix(&mut self, prefix: &[Value], metered: bool) -> Result<Vec<Value>, RuntimeError> {
   352	        if metered { self.meter.heap(DisclosedKeys(prefix).heap())?; }
   353	        Ok(prefix.to_vec())
   354	    }
   355	
   356	    fn push_branch_lane<'a>(&mut self, lanes: &mut Vec<Lane<'a>>, lane: Lane<'a>, metered: bool, site: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   357	        if metered {
   358	            self.meter.at(site);
   359	            if !self.read_charge(MeterKind::Steps, 1)? { return Ok(()); }
   360	            word_heap::push(&mut self.meter, lanes, lane)
   361	        } else { lanes.push(lane); Ok(()) }
   362	    }
   363	
   364	    fn push_branch_head(meter: &mut crate::meter::Meter, heap: &mut BinaryHeap<MergeHead>, head: MergeHead, metered: bool) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   365	        if metered {
   366	            let additional = word_heap::grow::<MergeHead>(meter, heap.len(), heap.capacity())?;
   367	            if additional != 0 { heap.reserve_exact(additional); }
   368	        }
   369	        heap.push(head);
   370	        Ok(())
   371	    }
   372	
   373	    fn charge_branch_position(&mut self, lane: &Lane<'_>, row: &Value) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   374	        if !lane.metered { return Ok(()); }
   375	        let index = &self.schema.tables[&lane.scan.table].indexes[&lane.scan.index];
   376	        let copied = index.components.iter().skip(lane.prefix.len()).filter_map(|part| part.column())
   377	            .filter_map(|name| row.field(name).ok()).map(|value| DisclosedKey(value).copy_heap()).sum::<u64>();
   378	        let appended = if index.components.len() - lane.prefix.len() < lane.position_len {
   379	            row.field("id").map_or(0, |value| DisclosedKey(value).copy_heap())
   380	        } else { 0 };
   381	        self.meter.heap(word_heap::slots::<Value>(lane.position_len) + copied + appended)
   382	    }
   383	
   384	    fn charge_branch_page(&mut self, first: &Scan, site: &str, descriptor: &str, page: &[Value], next: Option<&Vec<Value>>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
   385	        let table = &self.schema.tables[&first.table];
   386	        let index = &table.indexes[&first.index];
   387	        let position_len = index.components.len() - first.prefix.len()
   388	            + usize::from(index.components.last().and_then(|part| part.column()).map(String::as_str) != Some("id"));
   389	        let shaped = self.page_plan.shapes.values().any(|shape| shape.page == site);
   390	        let mut max_key = 0;
   391	        let mut shape_bytes = if shaped { word_heap::vec_growth::<Vec<Value>>(page.len()) } else { 0 };
   392	        for row in page {
   393	            let copied = index.components.iter().skip(first.prefix.len()).filter_map(|part| part.column())
   394	                .filter_map(|name| row.field(name).ok()).map(|value| DisclosedKey(value).copy_heap()).sum::<u64>();
   395	            let appended = if index.components.len() - first.prefix.len() < position_len {
   396	                row.field("id").map_or(0, |value| DisclosedKey(value).copy_heap())
   397	            } else { 0 };
   398	            let bytes = word_heap::slots::<Value>(position_len) + copied + appended;
   399	            max_key = max_key.max(bytes);
   400	            if shaped { shape_bytes += word_heap::vec_growth::<Value>(position_len) + copied + appended; }
   401	        }
   402	        // A meter frontier can continue an empty page, and may be wider than
   403	        // every emitted key. Cursor encoding must reserve that actual key.
   404	        if let Some(key) = next { max_key = max_key.max(DisclosedKeys(key).heap()); }
   405	        let trims = self.page_plan.shapes.iter().filter(|(_, shape)| shape.page == site).enumerate().map(|(n, (name, _))| {
   406	            word_heap::text(name) + max_key
   407	                + word_heap::insertion::<String, super::super::shaping::ShapePosition>(self.shape_positions.len() + n)
   408	        }).sum::<u64>();
   409	        self.meter.heap(shape_bytes + trims + word_heap::insertion::<String, PagePosition>(self.pages.len())
   410	            + 3 * (word_heap::text(site) + word_heap::text(&first.table) + word_heap::text(descriptor))
   411	            + next.map_or(0, |key| 2 * DisclosedKeys(key).heap())
   412	            + word_heap::cursor_build(table, index, first.prefix.len(), if next.is_some() || shaped { max_key } else { 0 },
   413	                word_heap::text(&first.table) + word_heap::text(descriptor)))
   414	    }
   415	
   416	    /// The lane's merge position for a row: its index components after the
   417	    /// lane's prefix, then `id` (the binder checks every lane has the same
   418	    /// position columns).
   419	    fn branch_position(&self, lane: &Lane<'_>, row: &Value) -> Vec<Value> {
   420	        let index = &self.schema.tables[&lane.scan.table].indexes[&lane.scan.index];
   421	        let mut key = Vec::with_capacity(lane.position_len);
   422	        key.extend(index.components.iter().skip(lane.prefix.len()).filter_map(|component| component.value(row)));
   423	        if key.len() < lane.position_len { key.push(row.field("id").cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null)); }
   424	        key
   425	    }
   426	}
   427	
   428	/// A branch token's scope: a digest of every lane's evaluated pins, in lane
   429	/// order. The lanes' prefixes together can exceed the token's 4096 bytes where
   430	/// each alone fits (GPT round 4, finding 3); the digest binds them all in 64.

codex
The patch retains fixed grant probes as residual filters and makes vacancy scans run the original read rule. I’m now checking whether core validation enforces those restrictions independently and whether cut paths can report false completion.
