   619	            // is non-unique and need not repeat id as a suffix.
   620	            && plain && (total_unique || prefix_total_unique
   621	                || index.components.last().and_then(|component| component.column()).is_some_and(|c| c == "id"))
   622	            && authority
   623	    })
   624	}
   625	
   626	/// Exhaustion is observable only when the authored read rule itself contains
   627	/// a top-level absence check on an exact required point keyed by this row's
   628	/// intrinsic id. The lane stays private and evaluates the complete rule.
   629	fn vacancy_branch(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane) -> bool {
   630	    if lane.source.is_some() || lane.scan.filter.is_some() || !lane.scan.prefix.is_empty() { return false; }
   631	    let Some(outer) = schema.table(&lane.scan.table) else { return false };
   632	    if outer.sync.is_some() { return false; }
   633	    outer.rules.read.disjuncts().iter().any(|rule| {
   634	        let RuleExpr::Not(inner) = rule else { return false };
   635	        let RuleExpr::Exists { table, index, key, equalities, .. } = inner.as_ref() else { return false };
   636	        let Some(target) = schema.table(table) else { return false };
   637	        let Some(point) = target.index(index) else { return false };
   638	        equalities.is_empty() && key == &[RuleTerm::Field("id".into())]
   639	            && point.unique && point.is_plain() && point.components.len() == 1
   640	            && point.components[0].column().is_some_and(|column|
   641	                target.columns.get(column).is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()))
   642	    })
   643	}
   644	
   645	/// An intrinsic-id membership lane retains id as its logical merge position
   646	/// even though the physical byId scan pins that sole component.
   647	pub fn membership_point_id(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane) -> bool {
   648	    if !lane.membership() || lane.scan.range.is_some() { return false; }
   649	    let Some(table) = schema.table(&lane.scan.table) else { return false };
   650	    let Some(index) = table.index(&lane.scan.index) else { return false };
   651	    let Some(Expr::Field { base, name: source_column }) = lane.distinct.as_ref() else { return false };
   652	    if !matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(binding) if binding == &lane.binding) { return false; }
   653	    lane.scan.prefix.len() == index.components.len()
   654	        && index.components.last().and_then(|part| part.column()).is_some_and(|column| column == "id")
   655	        && membership_outer_column(schema, lane, source_column) == Some("id")
   656	}
   657	
   658	/// The source is readable under its fixed pins, and its row is the exact
   659	/// witness for the outer rule branch that the dynamic prefix opens. A cap on
   660	/// the fixed source group is one admission proof; a total point witness is
   661	/// another. The latter bounds each witness, while the shared read meter safely
   662	/// cuts the otherwise-unbounded inverted discovery before any row is emitted.
   663	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#15 — membership discovery proves each parent range without treating point uniqueness as a global membership cap.
   664	fn membership_branch(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane) -> bool {
   665	    membership_witness(schema, lane, false)
   666	}
   667	
   668	/// A null discovered value may open a target lane only when the matching
   669	/// point witness identifies its row without that nullable component. A by-id
   670	/// probe with `parent = null` does; a nullable natural unique does not.
   671	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#14 — fixed-id witnesses preserve nullable residual equality without treating nullable natural keys as points.
   672	pub(crate) fn membership_null_distinct(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane) -> bool {
   673	    membership_witness(schema, lane, true)
   674	}
   675	
   676	/// Whether PAGE v1's validator would admit this already-valid membership
   677	/// lane. Capability derivation uses the historical predicate exactly, so
   678	/// capped §14 plans remain v1-compatible while every newly admitted shape is
   679	/// fenced by `branch-membership-v2`.
   680	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#15 — PAGE2 capability negotiation preserves the v1 admission boundary.
   681	pub fn membership_v1(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane) -> bool {
   682	    if !lane.membership() { return false; }
   683	    let Some(source) = lane.source.as_ref().and_then(crate::ir::LaneSource::scan) else { return false };
   684	    let Some(Expr::Field { base, name: source_column }) = lane.distinct.as_ref() else { return false };
   685	    if !matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(name) if name == &lane.binding) { return false; }
   686	    let Some(source_table) = schema.table(&source.table) else { return false };
   687	    let Some(source_index) = source_table.index(&source.index) else { return false };
   688	    let next_component = source.prefix.len() < source_index.components.len()
   689	        && source_index.components.get(source.prefix.len()).and_then(|part| part.column()) == Some(source_column);
   690	    let one_row_source = source.prefix.len() == source_index.components.len()
   691	        && source_index.unique && source_index.is_plain()
   692	        && source_index.components.iter().all(|part| part.column().and_then(|column| source_table.columns.get(column))
   693	            .is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()));
   694	    if source.existence || (!next_component && !one_row_source) || !scan_visibility(schema, source).decided_true { return false; }
   695	    let Bound::Literal(take @ 1..) = source.take else { return false };
   696	    let prefix_has = |field: &String| source_index.components.iter().take(source.prefix.len())
   697	        .any(|part| part.column() == Some(field));
   698	    let cap = source_table.constraints.iter().filter_map(|constraint| match constraint {
   699	        crate::schema::Constraint::Cap { by, max } if by.iter().all(prefix_has) && !source_table.cap_exempts_null(by) => Some(*max),
   700	        _ => None,
   701	    }).min();
   702	    if cap.is_none_or(|cap| cap > take && !one_row_source) { return false; }
   703	    membership_outer_column(schema, lane, source_column).is_some_and(|column| column != "id")
   704	}
   705	
   706	fn membership_outer_column<'a>(schema: &'a Schema, lane: &'a crate::ir::MergeLane, source_column: &str) -> Option<&'a str> {
   707	    let table = schema.table(&lane.scan.table)?;
   708	    let index = table.index(&lane.scan.index)?;
   709	    let mut outer = None;
   710	    for (part, key) in index.components.iter().zip(&lane.scan.prefix) {
   711	        let (Some(column), Some(expr)) = (part.column(), key.scalar()) else { return None };
   712	        if matches!(expr, Expr::Field { base, name } if matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(binding) if binding == &lane.binding) && name == source_column)
   713	            && outer.replace(column).is_some() { return None; }
   714	    }
   715	    outer.map(String::as_str)
   716	}
   717	
   718	fn membership_witness(schema: &Schema, lane: &crate::ir::MergeLane, null_distinct: bool) -> bool {
   719	    let Some(source) = lane.source.as_ref().and_then(crate::ir::LaneSource::scan) else { return false };
   720	    let Some(Expr::Field { base, name: source_column }) = lane.distinct.as_ref() else { return false };
   721	    if !matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(name) if name == &lane.binding) { return false; }
   722	    let Some(source_table) = schema.table(&source.table) else { return false };
   723	    let Some(source_index) = source_table.index(&source.index) else { return false };
   724	    let next_component = source.prefix.len() < source_index.components.len()
   725	        && source_index.components.get(source.prefix.len()).and_then(|part| part.column()) == Some(source_column);
   726	    // A fully pinned required unique identifies at most one source row, so
   727	    // any of that row's columns can be the discovered value. The source need
   728	    // not walk the distinct column as a following index component.
   729	    let one_row_source = source.prefix.len() == source_index.components.len()
   730	        && source_index.unique && source_index.is_plain()
   731	        && source_index.components.iter().all(|part| part.column().and_then(|column| source_table.columns.get(column))
   732	            .is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()));
   733	    if source.existence || (!next_component && !one_row_source)
   734	        || !scan_visibility(schema, source).decided_true { return false; }
   735	    // An applicable declaration can compile to a literal source bound. An
   736	    // exact total point may instead use the ordinary read meter: discovery
   737	    // remains fenced until that metered scan completes.
   738	    if !matches!(source.take, Bound::Meter | Bound::Literal(1..)) { return false; }
   739	    let prefix_has = |field: &String| source_index.components.iter().take(source.prefix.len())
   740	        .any(|part| part.column() == Some(field));
   741	    let capped_source_group = source_table.constraints.iter().any(|constraint| match constraint {
   742	        crate::schema::Constraint::Cap { by, max } if by.iter().all(prefix_has) && !source_table.cap_exempts_null(by) => Some(*max),
   743	        _ => None,
   744	    }.is_some());
   745	    let Some(outer_table) = schema.table(&lane.scan.table) else { return false };
   746	    let Some(outer_index) = outer_table.index(&lane.scan.index) else { return false };
   747	    let mut outer_column: Option<&String> = None;
   748	    for (part, key) in outer_index.components.iter().zip(&lane.scan.prefix) {
   749	        let (Some(column), Some(expr)) = (part.column(), key.scalar()) else { return false };
   750	        if matches!(expr, Expr::Field { base, name } if matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(binding) if binding == &lane.binding) && name == source_column)
   751	            && outer_column.replace(column).is_some() { return false; }
   752	    }
   753	    let Some(outer_column) = outer_column else { return false };
   754	    let fixed = |column: &String| source_index.components.iter().zip(&source.prefix)
   755	        .find_map(|(part, key)| (part.column() == Some(column)).then(|| key.scalar()).flatten())
   756	        .or_else(|| source.filter.as_deref().and_then(|filter| required_source_equality(filter, column)));
   757	    let pins = scan_pinned(outer_table, &lane.scan);
   758	    let pinned = |column: &str| pins.iter().find_map(|(name, value)| (name.as_str() == column).then_some(*value));
   759	    membership_rule(&outer_table.rules.read, &|rule| {
   760	        let RuleExpr::Exists { table, index, key, equalities, .. } = rule else { return false };
   761	        let Some(probe_index) = source_table.index(index) else { return false };
   762	        if table != &source.table || !probe_index.unique || !probe_index.is_plain() || probe_index.components.len() != key.len() { return false; }
   763	        let total_point = probe_index.components.iter().all(|part| part.column()
   764	            .and_then(|column| source_table.columns.get(column)).is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()));
   765	        if !one_row_source && !capped_source_group && !total_point { return false; }
   766	        let matches = |column: &String, term: &RuleTerm| if column == source_column { matches!(term, RuleTerm::Field(field) if field == outer_column) }
   767	            else { fixed(column).is_some_and(|expr| match term {
   768	                // @ref LLP 3000.036.001#17 — an exact target pin fixes one component of a multi-column point witness.
   769	                RuleTerm::Field(field) => pinned(field).is_some_and(|pin| pin == expr),
   770	                _ => term_matches_expr(term, expr),
   771	            }) };
   772	        if !probe_index.components.iter().zip(key).all(|(part, term)| part.column().is_some_and(|column| matches(column, term)))
   773	            || !equalities.iter().all(|(column, term)| matches(column, term)) { return false; }
   774	        !null_distinct || probe_index.components.iter().all(|part| part.column().is_some_and(|column| column != source_column
   775	            && fixed(column).is_some_and(|expr| matches!(expr, Expr::Input(Input::Viewer)) || fixed_non_null_literal(expr))))
   776	    }, &|rule| rule_true(rule, &lane.scan.table, outer_table, &pinned)
   777	        || lane.scan.filter.as_deref().is_some_and(|filter| fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, filter, rule, &pins)))
   778	}
   779	
   780	/// A generated membership lane may retain a second, prefix-fixed point grant
   781	/// as a residual predicate.  Recognize only the exact bound point expression:
   782	/// this proves that every row emitted by the lane satisfies that conjunct,
   783	/// while a missing, reordered or merely similar probe keeps the merge out.
   784	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — one fixed grant may guard one varying per-row grant.
   785	fn fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema: &Schema, filter: &Expr, rule: &RuleExpr,
   786	    pins: &[(&String, &Expr)]) -> bool {
   787	    if let Expr::Binary { op: crate::ir::BinaryOp::And, left, right } = filter {
   788	        return fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, left, rule, pins)
   789	            || fixed_filter_requires_rule(schema, right, rule, pins);
   790	    }
   791	    let (Expr::Exists(read), RuleExpr::Exists { table, index, key, equalities, .. }) = (filter, rule) else { return false };
   792	    let Some(target) = schema.table(table) else { return false };
   793	    let Some(point) = target.index(index) else { return false };
   794	    equalities.is_empty() && target.through.is_none() && target.not_before.is_none() && !target.is_expiring()
   795	        && point.unique && point.is_plain() && point.components.iter().all(|part| part.column()
   796	            .and_then(|column| target.columns.get(column)).is_some_and(|kind| !kind.is_optional()))
   797	        && schema.rule_input_is_readable(table, index, key)
   798	        && &read.table == table && &read.index == index && read.key.len() == key.len()
   799	        && read.key.iter().zip(key).all(|(expression, term)| rule_term_expression(term, expression, pins))
   800	}
   801	
   802	fn rule_term_expression(term: &RuleTerm, expression: &Expr, pins: &[(&String, &Expr)]) -> bool {
   803	    match (term, expression) {
   804	        (RuleTerm::Viewer, Expr::Input(Input::Viewer)) => true,
   805	        (RuleTerm::Literal(value), Expr::Literal(actual)) => value == actual,
   806	        (RuleTerm::Field(field), expression) => pins.iter().find_map(|(name, value)|
   807	            (name.as_str() == field).then_some(*value)).is_some_and(|value| value == expression),
   808	        _ => false,
   809	    }
   810	}
   811	
   812	/// A witness supplies one conjunct; every other conjunct must already hold
   813	/// by the lane's pins. Distribution does not drop a status/role condition.
   814	fn membership_rule(rule: &RuleExpr, witness: &impl Fn(&RuleExpr) -> bool, pinned: &impl Fn(&RuleExpr) -> bool) -> bool {
   815	    match rule {
   816	        RuleExpr::Or(parts) => parts.iter().any(|part| membership_rule(part, witness, pinned)),
   817	        RuleExpr::And(parts) => parts.iter().enumerate().any(|(at, part)|
   818	            membership_rule(part, witness, pinned) && parts.iter().enumerate().all(|(other, rule)| at == other || pinned(rule))),
   819	        _ => witness(rule),
   820	    }
   821	}
   822	
   823	/// A generated membership discovery filter may retain an exact fixed probe
   824	/// component after its capped prefix (`id = 'm1'`, for example). Only a bare
   825	/// equality in the filter's conjunction is a necessary fact about every
   826	/// discovered row; alternatives and other implications remain unsupported.
   827	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#14 — fixed membership witness columns may be proved by exact source residuals.
   828	fn required_source_equality<'a>(filter: &'a Expr, column: &str) -> Option<&'a Expr> {
   829	    match filter {
   830	        Expr::Binary { op: crate::ir::BinaryOp::And, left, right } =>
   831	            required_source_equality(left, column).or_else(|| required_source_equality(right, column)),
   832	        Expr::Binary { op: crate::ir::BinaryOp::Eq, left, right } if source_field(left, column) => Some(right),
   833	        Expr::Binary { op: crate::ir::BinaryOp::Eq, left, right } if source_field(right, column) => Some(left),
   834	        _ => None,
   835	    }
   836	}
   837	
   838	fn source_field(expr: &Expr, column: &str) -> bool {
   839	    matches!(expr, Expr::Field { base, name }
   840	        if name == column && matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(binding) if binding == "$filter"))
   841	}
   842	
   843	fn term_matches_expr(term: &RuleTerm, expr: &Expr) -> bool {
   844	    match (term, expr) { (RuleTerm::Viewer, Expr::Input(Input::Viewer)) => true,
   845	        (RuleTerm::Literal(value), expr) => literal_matches_expr(value, expr), _ => false }
   846	}
   847	
   848	fn literal_matches_expr(value: &Value, expr: &Expr) -> bool {
   849	    match expr {
   850	        Expr::Literal(other) => value == other,
   851	        Expr::Unary { op: crate::ir::UnaryOp::Neg, .. } => matches!(value, Value::Int(expected)
   852	            if expr_int_literal(expr).is_some_and(|actual| actual == i128::from(*expected))),
   853	        _ => false,
   854	    }
   855	}
   856	
   857	fn expr_int_literal(expr: &Expr) -> Option<i128> {
   858	    match expr {
   859	        Expr::Literal(Value::Int(value)) => Some(i128::from(*value)),
   860	        Expr::Unary { op: crate::ir::UnaryOp::Neg, value } => expr_int_literal(value).map(|value| -value),
   861	        _ => None,
   862	    }
   863	}

   372	impl Scan {
   373	    /// True only for an ordinary index ordered by this column after its pins.
   374	    /// Kept shared by execution and the cost certificate.
   375	    pub fn orders_field(&self, schema: &crate::schema::Schema, field: &str) -> bool {
   376	        let Some(index) = schema.table(&self.table).and_then(|table| table.index(&self.index)) else { return false; };
   377	        // Expiring maintained totals are refreshed while reading. Their
   378	        // stored index value does not order the live value returned today.
   379	        if field == "value" && schema.maintain_for(&self.table).is_some_and(|maintain|
   380	            schema.table(&maintain.source).is_some_and(|source| source.expire.is_some())) { return false; }

   382	            && matches!(index.components.get(self.prefix.len()), Some(crate::schema::IndexComponent::Column(column)) if column == field)

   395	/// A merge owns its lane discovery and ordered scans; no materialized union.

   258	
   259	    /// Whether an `exists` probe's target row is structurally readable by the
   260	    /// probing viewer (public target, or the read rule is proven true for the
   261	    /// exact indexed row being probed). Copied from Snapback 2: the proof is
   262	    /// structural on purpose, so a hidden probe never leaks by its result.
   263	    pub fn rule_input_is_readable(&self, table_name: &str, index_name: &str, key: &[RuleTerm]) -> bool {
   264	        let Some(table) = self.table(table_name) else { return false };
   265	        if table.rules.read.is_uniformly_readable() {
   266	            return true;
   267	        }
   268	        let Some(index) = table.index(index_name) else { return false };
   269	        if !index.is_plain() {
   270	            return false;
   271	        }
   272	        let columns = index.columns();
   273	        prove_probe_row_readable(&table.rules.read, table_name, index_name, &columns, key)
   274	    }
   275	}
   276	
   277	fn prove_probe_row_readable(rule: &RuleExpr, table: &str, index: &str, index_columns: &[String], probe_key: &[RuleTerm]) -> bool {
   278	    let substitute = |term: &RuleTerm| match term {
   279	        RuleTerm::Field(field) => index_columns.iter().position(|column| column == field).and_then(|position| probe_key.get(position)).cloned(),
   280	        RuleTerm::OldField(_) | RuleTerm::NextField(_) => None,
   281	        other => Some(other.clone()),
   282	    };
   283	    match rule {
   284	        RuleExpr::Public { .. } | RuleExpr::Allow => true,
   285	        RuleExpr::Eq(left, right) => substitute(left).zip(substitute(right)).is_some_and(|(left, right)| left == right),
   286	        RuleExpr::And(rules) => !rules.is_empty() && rules.iter().all(|rule| prove_probe_row_readable(rule, table, index, index_columns, probe_key)),
   287	        RuleExpr::Or(rules) => rules.iter().any(|rule| prove_probe_row_readable(rule, table, index, index_columns, probe_key)),
   288	        RuleExpr::Exists { table: target, index: target_index, key, equalities, .. } if target == table && target_index == index && equalities.is_empty() => {
   289	            key.iter().map(substitute).collect::<Option<Vec<_>>>().is_some_and(|key| key == probe_key)
   290	        }
   291	        RuleExpr::Deny | RuleExpr::Compare(..) | RuleExpr::Ne(_, _) | RuleExpr::Not(_) | RuleExpr::Exists { .. } | RuleExpr::Created { .. } => false,
   292	    }
   293	}

   301	    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "BTreeMap::is_empty")]

   303	    /// Field authority and visibility, independent of the row update grant.

   305	    pub written_by: BTreeMap<String, FieldAuthority>,
   306	    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "std::ops::Not::not")]

   947	/// An authored equality may already be the right authorization scope while a
   948	/// named order index leaves it as a residual filter. Print the two exact edits
   949	/// together: the compatible declaration and the same query selecting it.
   950	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — equality-filter advice is mechanically coherent even when it remains advisory.
   951	fn equality_filter_index_rewrite(table: &snapback4_core::schema::Table, scan: &snapback4_core::ir::Scan,
   952	    unpinned: &[String], leading: &[String], declaration: Option<&str>) -> Option<String> {
   953	    use snapback4_core::ir::{BinaryOp, Expr};
   954	    fn pins(expr: &Expr, column: &str) -> bool {
   955	        match expr {
   956	            Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::And, left, right } => pins(left, column) || pins(right, column),
   957	            Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Eq, left, right } => field(left, column) && independent(right)
   958	                || field(right, column) && independent(left),
   959	            _ => false,
   960	        }
   961	    }
   962	    fn field(expr: &Expr, column: &str) -> bool {
   963	        matches!(expr, Expr::Field { base, name } if name == column
   964	            && matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(binding) if binding == "$filter"))
   965	    }
   966	    fn independent(expr: &Expr) -> bool {
   967	        match expr {
   968	            Expr::Field { base, .. } => !matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(binding) if binding == "$filter") && independent(base),
   969	            Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => independent(left) && independent(right),
   970	            Expr::Unary { value, .. } => independent(value),
   971	            Expr::Array(values) => values.iter().all(independent),
   972	            Expr::Object(fields) => fields.values().all(independent),
   973	            Expr::Builtin { args, .. } => args.iter().all(independent),
   974	            Expr::Get(_) | Expr::Exists(_) | Expr::Scan(_) | Expr::Merge(_) | Expr::Block { .. } => false,
   975	            _ => true,
   976	        }
   977	    }
   978	    let filter = scan.filter.as_deref()?;
   979	    if unpinned.is_empty() || leading.len() > 8 || !unpinned.iter().all(|column| pins(filter, column)) { return None; }
   980	    let title = |value: &str| { let mut chars = value.chars(); chars.next()
   981	        .map(|first| first.to_uppercase().collect::<String>() + chars.as_str()).unwrap_or_default() };
   982	    let stem = leading.iter().filter(|column| column.as_str() != "id").map(|column| title(column)).collect::<String>();
   983	    if stem.is_empty() { return None; }
   984	    let mut name = format!("by{stem}");
   985	    let mut suffix = 2;
   986	    while table.indexes.contains_key(&name) {
   987	        name = format!("by{stem}{suffix}"); suffix += 1;
   988	    }
   989	    let declaration = declaration?;
   990	    let old = format!(" by {}", scan.index);
   991	    if !declaration.contains(&old) { return None; }
   992	    let rewritten = declaration.replacen(&old, &format!(" by {name}"), 1);
   993	    Some(format!("on table {} add `by {name}: {}`; use the same index in the rewritten query:\n```quarry\n{rewritten}\n```", scan.table, leading.join(", ")))
   994	}
   995	
   996	fn matching_merge_rewrite(schema: &Schema, scan: &snapback4_core::ir::Scan, table: &snapback4_core::schema::Table) -> Option<String> {
   997	    use snapback4_core::{ir::IndexKeyExpr, schema::{RuleExpr, RuleTerm}};
   998	    if !scan.prefix.iter().any(|key| matches!(key, IndexKeyExpr::WordPrefix(_))) { return None; }
   999	    // @ref LLP 3000.007#18.1 — a membership conjunct supplies lanes when their scope decides the whole rule.
  1000	    fn conjuncts(rule: &RuleExpr) -> Vec<&RuleExpr> {
  1001	        match rule { RuleExpr::And(terms) => terms.iter().flat_map(conjuncts).collect(), other => vec![other] }
  1002	    }
  1003	    conjuncts(&table.rules.read).into_iter().find_map(|rule| {
  1004	        let RuleExpr::Exists { table: members, index: membership_index, key, .. } = rule else { return None; };
  1005	        let membership = schema.table(members)?;
  1006	        let columns = membership.index(membership_index)?.columns();
  1007	        let principal = key.iter().position(|term| matches!(term, RuleTerm::Viewer))?;
  1008	        let scope: Vec<_> = key.iter().filter_map(|term| if let RuleTerm::Field(field) = term { Some(field.clone()) } else { None }).collect();
  1009	        let deciding = snapback4_core::engine::rules::deciding_fields(&scan.table, table, &table.rules.read);
  1010	        if !deciding.iter().all(|field| scope.contains(field)) { return None; }
  1011	        let fields = table.index(&scan.index)?.word_component()?.1;
  1012	        let (_, words_index) = table.indexes.iter().find(|(_, index)| index.word_component().is_some_and(|(position, text)| index.columns()[..position] == scope && text == fields))?;
  1013	        let _ = membership.indexes.iter().find(|(_, index)| index.is_plain() && index.columns().first() == columns.get(principal) && index.columns().last().is_some_and(|column| column == "id"))?;
  1014	        let order = words_index.columns().into_iter().skip(scope.len()).map(|column| format!(".{column}")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
  1015	        Some(format!("{} matching q where {} last {} by {order} after c — one verdict per scope; complete while every head opens and the page fits in one read; or read the page on the device", scan.table, table.rules.read.quarry(), scan.take.maximum()))
  1016	    })
  1017	}

  1030	            let column = key.column.as_ref().or_else(|| index.and_then(|index| index.columns.get(i)))?;

+/-- PAGE5's fixed half of a fixed-plus-varying grant: after substituting the
+page pins, this is one timeless, required exact point whose ordinary read
+rule discloses the same answer to the caller. -/
+def fixedPointGuard (sch : Schema) (outer : TableDecl)
+    (pins : List (String × Expr)) : Rule → Bool
+  | r@(.probe t point key) => match sch.table? t with
+    | some source =>
+      let columns := key.map (·.1)
+      key.length == point.length
+        && point.eraseDups.length == point.length
+        && point.all columns.contains
+        && (point == ["id"] || source.uniques.contains point)
+        && point.all (fun name => name == "id" || source.cols.any (fun col => col.name == name && !col.nullable))
+        && source.notBefore.isNone && source.expire.isNone
+        && r.usableGuard sch outer pins
+    | none => false
+  | _ => false
+
+/-- PAGE5's varying half has exactly one outer-row key component and otherwise
+uses PAGE's existing readable, total membership witness. -/
+def varyingPointGrant (sch : Schema) (pins : List (String × Expr))
+    (order : List String) : Rule → Bool
+  | .probe t point key => membershipEligible sch t point key
+      && (membershipColumns key).any fun pair =>
+        !pins.any (·.1 == pair.2)
+          && (!order.contains pair.2 || pair.2 == "id" && order == ["id"])
+          && laneFits order [(pair.2, .viewer)] pins
+  | _ => false
+
 /-- Each positive alternative either has a prefix-decided rule, its own
 readable guard, or one exact membership witness with every remaining
 conjunct true by its pins. -/
@@ -593,6 +622,9 @@ def compoundDecided (sch : Schema) (td : TableDecl) (pins : List (String × Expr

                   && laneFits order ((pair.2, .viewer) :: extra) pins
               | _ => false
+            | [a, b] =>
+                (fixedPointGuard sch td ps a && varyingPointGrant sch ps order b)
+                  || (fixedPointGuard sch td ps b && varyingPointGrant sch ps order a)
             | _ => false)
 
 
@@ -621,6 +653,17 @@ def scopedWitnessDecided (sch : Schema) (td : TableDecl)
               && laneFits order [(pair.2, .viewer)] pins
     | _ => false
 
+/-- PAGE5's explicit vacancy: a top-level negated probe of one required
+exact point by the focal row's intrinsic id. The native lane still checks the
+whole read rule per row; this predicate governs only its exhaustion verdict. -/
+def vacancyDecided (sch : Schema) (td : TableDecl) : Bool :=
+  td.read.disjuncts.any fun r => match r with
+    | .not (.probe t [point] [(key, .col .self "id")]) =>
+        point == key && (sch.table? t).any fun source =>
+          source.uniques.contains [point]
+            && (source.cols.find? (·.name == point)).all (!·.nullable)
+    | _ => false

 which stay seeks (`Requests::read`; GPT round 3, finding 3). -/

     let witnessScope := guards && pins.all (fun p => p.2.total) && scopedWitnessDecided sch td pins fs order

+def page5Product : Schema := { tables := [
+  { name := "items", cols := [c "owner", c "helper", c "status", c "at"]
+    read := .and
+      (.or (.eq (self "owner") .viewer) (.eq (self "helper") .viewer))
+      (.or (.eq (self "status") (.lit (T "open"))) (.eq (self "status") (.lit (T "active")))) }] }

    23	/// Only turn an implicit whole-group read into a page when the original
    24	/// prefix has a stored bound. A party cap does not bound a staff's all-row
    25	/// branch. The original bound applies to the union, rather than each party.
    26	fn bounded_page(tables: &Tables, requests: &Requests, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<ReadExpr> {
    27	    if read.range.is_some() { return Some(read.clone()); }
    28	    let table = tables.get(&read.table)?;
    29	    let mut planned = read.clone();
    30	    let mut probe = requests.clone();
    31	    probe.read(tables, &mut planned, false);
    32	    let pinned: Vec<String> = planned.keys.iter().filter_map(|key| key.column.clone()).collect();
    33	    let cap = table.decl.caps.iter().filter(|cap| cap.by.iter().all(|column| pinned.contains(column))
    34	        && !cap.by.iter().any(|column| table.decl.columns.iter().any(|field| &field.name == column
    35	            && field.on_delete.as_ref().is_some_and(|(action, _)| action == &OnDelete::SetNull))))
    36	        .map(|cap| cap.max).chain(table.indexes.iter().filter(|index| table.total_unique(index))
    37	            .filter(|index| index.columns.iter().all(|column| pinned.contains(column))).map(|_| 1)).min()?;
    38	    if u64::from(cap) > snapback4_core::Ceilings::default().examined { return None; }
    39	    let mut page = read.clone();
    40	    page.range = Some(RangeSpec { order: Order::Asc, take: cap, cursor: None, site: read.site.clone() });
    41	    Some(page)
    42	}

   100	/// An exact positive where witness that is itself a whole read-rule disjunct
   101	/// restricts W to that audience. Enumerate it directly; other audiences cannot
   102	/// contribute rows to W, including the system's rule bypass.
   103	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#17 — author-scoped pages retain their exact witness.
   104	fn scoped_membership(tables: &Tables, synthesized: &std::collections::BTreeSet<(String, String)>, requests: &mut Requests, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<MergeExpr> {
   105	    let table = tables.get(&read.table)?;
   106	    let rule = read_rule(table)?;
   107	    let mut leaves = Vec::new(); disjuncts(rule, &mut leaves);
   108	    let mut predicates = Vec::new(); intent::split(*read.filter.clone()?, &mut predicates);
   109	    for (at, predicate) in predicates.iter().enumerate() {
   110	        if !matches!(predicate, Expr::Exists { .. }) || !leaves.iter().any(|leaf|
   111	            crate::print::expression(leaf) == crate::print::expression(predicate)) { continue; }
   112	        let rest: Vec<_> = predicates.iter().enumerate().filter(|(n, _)| *n != at).map(|(_, p)| p.clone()).collect();
   113	        if !rest.iter().all(total) { continue; }
   114	        let mut narrowed = read.clone(); narrowed.filter = intent::conjunction(rest);
   115	        let mut local = tables.clone();
   116	        local.get_mut(&read.table)?.decl.rules.iter_mut().find(|rule| rule.effect == Effect::Read)?.value = predicate.clone();
   117	        let mut candidate = requests.clone();
   118	        let mut merge = membership(&local, synthesized, &mut candidate, &narrowed)?;
   119	        // The system bypasses R, never W. Its whole-range descriptor retains
   120	        // the authored witness, and also binds all static target pins into
   121	        // the shared cursor scope. Other callers use exact discovery only.
   122	        for lane in &mut merge.lanes {
   123	            if lane.branch.as_deref() == Some(SYSTEM_LANE) {
   124	                let planned = lane.planned.as_mut()?;
   125	                let mut residual = planned.filter.take().map(|p| vec![*p]).unwrap_or_default();
   126	                residual.push(predicate.clone()); planned.filter = intent::conjunction(residual);
   127	            }
   128	            lane.admission = read.filter.clone();
   129	        }
   130	        *requests = candidate;
   131	        return Some(merge);
   132	    }
   133	    None
   134	}

   430	/// The branches of an undecided read, or `None` when the rule does not apply.
   431	fn branches(tables: &Tables, synthesized: &std::collections::BTreeSet<(String, String)>, requests: &Requests, read: &ReadExpr, grants: bool) -> Option<Vec<Branch>> {
   432	    let table = tables.get(&read.table)?;
   433	    if read.index.is_some() || !read.keys.is_empty() || read.matching.is_some() || !read.computed.is_empty() || read.cursor_prefix.is_some() { return None; }
   434	    if table.maintained.is_some() || table.through.is_some() || table.decl.ephemeral.is_some() || !table.captures.is_empty() || table.decl.public.is_some() { return None; }
   435	    let rule = read_rule(table)?;
   436	    // A page its own pins decide keeps its plan.
   437	    let mut probe = requests.clone();
   438	    let mut planned = read.clone();
   439	    probe.read(tables, &mut planned, false);
   440	    if decided(tables, &planned) { return None; }
   441	    // Each lane evaluates the predicate's parts on its own rows: they must be
   442	    // total, so moving an alternative into its own lane cannot raise a refusal
   443	    // that the original's short circuit skipped (`or (… and 1 / n = 1)`).
   444	    if read.filter.as_deref().is_some_and(|filter| !super::intent_compound::total_readable(tables, synthesized, table, filter)) { return None; }
   445	    let conjuncts = read.filter.as_ref().map(|filter| { let mut out = Vec::new(); intent::split(*filter.clone(), &mut out); out }).unwrap_or_default();
   446	    let alternatives = where_branches(table, &conjuncts);
   447	    // Case 1: every alternative of the one `or` in `where` decides the rule.
   448	    if let Some(alternatives) = &alternatives
   449	        && alternatives.iter().all(|alternative| {
   450	            let mut lane = read.clone();
   451	            lane.filter = intent::conjunction(alternative.clone());
   452	            let mut probe = requests.clone();
   453	            probe.plan_lane(tables, &mut lane);
   454	            decided(tables, &lane)
   455	        }) {
   456	        return Some(alternatives.iter().map(|alternative| (alternative.clone(), false)).collect());
   457	    }
   458	    // Case 2: the read rule is parties and page guards: readable grants, and
   459	    // disjuncts the page's own pins decide (LLP 3000.036 §16.3), which the
   460	    // binding check confirms with the core's `covering_grant`.
   461	    let mut disjunct_list = Vec::new();
   462	    disjuncts(rule, &mut disjunct_list);
   463	    if disjunct_list.len() < 2 { return None; }
   464	    let pinned: Vec<String> = conjuncts.iter().filter_map(|conjunct| intent::equality(conjunct, table).map(|(column, _)| column)).collect();
   465	    let mut parties = Vec::new();
   466	    for disjunct in &disjunct_list {
   467	        if let Some(column) = party(table, disjunct) { if !parties.contains(&column) { parties.push(column); } }
   468	        else if columns_within(disjunct, &pinned) { /* a guard: the grant lane reads it */ }
   469	        else { return None; }
   470	    }
   471	    // `exists` takes case 1 only: an `or` of party reads misses rows for the
   472	    // system, which every rule admits, and a guard lane has no existence form.
   473	    if parties.is_empty() || !grants { return None; }
   474	    let base = alternatives.unwrap_or_else(|| vec![conjuncts.clone()]);
   475	    // The grant lane, the read as written, is read for a caller its guards
   476	    // admit and for the system (which every rule admits); a rule of parties
   477	    // alone has no guard, so the lane is the system's only.
   478	    let mut out = vec![(conjuncts, true)];
   479	    for branch in &base {
   480	        for column in &parties {
   481	            let mut lane = vec![viewer_pin(column, &read.site)];
   482	            lane.extend(branch.iter().cloned());
   483	            out.push((lane, false));
   484	        }
   485	    }
   486	    (out.len() <= MAX_BRANCHES).then_some(out)
   487	}
   488	
   489	/// The bounded positive alternatives of the read's top-level conjunction.
   490	/// Each `or` contributes a Cartesian factor, so
   491	/// `(a = x or b = x) and (state = 'open' or state = 'held')` becomes four
   492	/// independently pinned lanes.  Keep the published eight-lane ceiling while
   493	/// allowing an exact query predicate to narrow a wider read-rule union.
   494	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#18 — exact positive query scopes may combine party and status alternatives.
   495	fn where_branches(table: &TableInfo, conjuncts: &[Expr]) -> Option<Vec<Vec<Expr>>> {
   496	    let mut branches = vec![Vec::new()];
   497	    let mut distributed = false;
   498	    for conjunct in conjuncts {
   499	        let mut alternatives = Vec::new();
   500	        disjuncts(conjunct, &mut alternatives);
   501	        distributed |= alternatives.len() > 1;
   502	        if branches.len().saturating_mul(alternatives.len()) > MAX_BRANCHES { return None; }
   503	        branches = branches.into_iter().flat_map(|prefix| alternatives.iter().map(move |alternative| {
   504	            let mut lane = prefix.clone();
   505	            intent::split(alternative.clone(), &mut lane);
   506	            lane
   507	        })).collect();
   508	    }
   509	    if !distributed || branches.len() < 2 || branches.iter().any(|lane| !lane.iter().any(|conjunct| pin(table, conjunct))) {
   510	        return None;
   511	    }
   512	    Some(branches)
   513	}
   514	
   515	/// An equality the planner turns into an index prefix. A nullable column is
   516	/// pinned only to `viewer`, never to a value that may be null, so a branch
   517	/// never seeks a null the original filter rejects.
   518	fn pin(table: &TableInfo, conjunct: &Expr) -> bool {
   519	    let Some((column, value)) = intent::equality(conjunct, table) else { return false };
   520	    column != "id" && table.columns.get(&column).is_some_and(|ty| !ty.optional || is_viewer(&value))
   521	}
   522	
   523	/// The planned read's prefix decides the read rule: every column the rule
   524	/// reads is pinned (with the self-witness proof), or the rule is true by the
   525	/// pins' form (the core's `rule_true`, and the Lean model's `Rule.byForm`).
   526	pub(super) fn decided(tables: &Tables, planned: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
   527	    let Some(table) = tables.get(&planned.table) else { return false };
   528	    if super::intent_lanes::prefix_decides_rule(tables, planned) { return true; }
   529	    read_rule(table).is_some_and(|rule| by_form(rule, planned))
   530	}
   531	
   532	/// `allow`, `.col = viewer` pinned to `viewer`, `.col = 'x'` pinned to `'x'`,
   533	/// and `or`/`and` of those.
   534	pub(super) fn by_form(rule: &Expr, planned: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
   535	    let pinned = |column: &str| planned.keys.iter().find(|key| key.column.as_deref() == Some(column)).map(|key| &key.value);
   536	    match rule {
   537	        Expr::Name { name, .. } if name == "allow" => true,
   538	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" => by_form(left, planned) || by_form(right, planned),
   539	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "and" => by_form(left, planned) && by_form(right, planned),
   540	        Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "=" => {
   541	            let (column, value) = match (intent::column(left), intent::column(right)) {
   542	                (Some(column), None) => (column, right.as_ref()),
   543	                (None, Some(column)) => (column, left.as_ref()),
   544	                _ => return false,
   545	            };
   546	            match (value, pinned(column)) {
   547	                (value, Some(pin)) if is_viewer(value) => is_viewer(pin),
   548	                (Expr::Literal { value, .. }, Some(Expr::Literal { value: pin, .. })) => value == pin && *value != Literal::Null,
   549	                _ => false,
   550	            }
   551	        }
   552	        _ => false,
   553	    }
   554	}
   555	
   556	pub(super) fn read_rule(table: &TableInfo) -> Option<&Expr> {

   563	fn disjuncts(value: &Expr, out: &mut Vec<Expr>) {
   564	    if let Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = value && op == "or" { disjuncts(left, out); disjuncts(right, out); }

   568	/// `.col = viewer` (either side) on a principal column of the row.
   569	fn party(table: &TableInfo, value: &Expr) -> Option<String> {
   570	    let Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = value else { return None };
   571	    if op != "=" { return None; }
   572	    let column = match (intent::column(left), intent::column(right)) {
   573	        (Some(column), None) if is_viewer(right) => column,
   574	        (None, Some(column)) if is_viewer(left) => column,
   575	        _ => return None,
   576	    };
   577	    (table.columns.get(column)?.kind == TypeKind::Principal).then(|| column.to_owned())

   582	pub(super) fn columns_within(value: &Expr, pinned: &[String]) -> bool {
   583	    let mut copy = value.clone();

   592	/// A bare `.` (the whole row) is not a pinned column.
   593	fn has_focal_without_member(value: &Expr) -> bool {
   594	    match value {

   596	        Expr::Member { base, .. } if matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Focal { .. }) => false,
   597	        Expr::Member { base, .. } | Expr::Unary { value: base, .. } => has_focal_without_member(base),
   598	        Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => has_focal_without_member(left) || has_focal_without_member(right),
   599	        Expr::Exists { read, .. } => read.keys.iter().any(|key| has_focal_without_member(&key.value)) || read.filter.as_deref().is_some_and(has_focal_without_member),

   601	        _ => false,
   602	    }
   603	}

   615	        Expr::Member { base, .. } => matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Focal { .. }),

    30	impl Rewrite {
    31	    pub fn name(&self) -> &'static str {

    38	            Self::Declarations => "declarations",

    41	    /// Whether the rewrite may change meter fields. Commuting operands
    42	    /// preserves cost only when neither hoists work: a read on the right of
    43	    /// a binary operator makes the binder spill the left value first ("Work
    44	    /// hoisted from the right cannot run before the left value",
    45	    /// `bind/program/expr.rs`), two more steps. Order of evaluation, not cost,
    46	    /// is the language's promise there.
    47	    pub fn costs(&self, p: &Program) -> bool {
    48	        match self {
    49	            Self::Declarations => false,
    50	            Self::Commute => match &p.body {
    51	                Body::Scalar(e) | Body::Write(_, e) | Body::Block(Block { ret: e, .. }) => reads(e),
    52	                _ => false,
    53	            },
    54	            _ => true,
    55	        }
    56	    }
    57	    /// Commuting a refused expression can reach the failing operand before
    58	    /// evaluating the other one. Preserve the refusal, not its executed cost.
    59	    // @ref LLP 3000.035#51-stage-1-a-generator-and-rust-against-rust-comparison — refused commutations compare semantics; the report counts omitted meters.
    60	    pub fn costs_for(&self, p: &Program, outcome: &Outcome) -> bool {
    61	        self.costs(p) || (matches!(self, Self::Commute) && outcome.refusal().is_some())
    62	    }
    63	    pub fn apply(&self, p: &Program) -> Option<Program> {
    64	        let mut q = p.clone();
    65	        match self {
    66	            Self::Declarations => q.reverse = !q.reverse,
    67	            Self::Let => q.bind_return = !q.bind_return,
    68	            Self::Commute | Self::Identity => {
    69	                let e = match &mut q.body {
    70	                    Body::Scalar(e)
    71	                    | Body::Write(Write::Insert | Write::Upsert | Write::Update, e) => e,
    72	                    Body::Block(b) => &mut b.ret,
    73	                    _ => return None,
    74	                };
    75	                if let Expr::Binary(op, a, b) = e {
    76	                    if matches!(self, Self::Commute) {
    77	                        if !matches!(op, Bin::Add | Bin::Mul | Bin::Eq) {
    78	                            return None;
    79	                        }
    80	                        std::mem::swap(a, b);
    81	                    } else if matches!(op, Bin::Add | Bin::Mul) {
    82	                        *a = Box::new(Expr::Identity(a.clone()));
    83	                        *b = Box::new(Expr::Identity(b.clone()));
    84	                        q.helper = true;
    85	                    } else {
    86	                        return None;
    87	                    }
    88	                } else {
    89	                    return None;
    90	                }
    91	            }

    93	                let is_or = matches!(self, Self::LazyOr);
    94	                if matches!(&q.body, Body::Scalar(Expr::Binary(op, left, _))
    95	                    if matches!((op, is_or), (Bin::Or, true) | (Bin::And, false)) && matches!(left.as_ref(), Expr::Bool(b) if *b == is_or))
    96	                {
    97	                    q.body = Body::Scalar(Expr::Bool(is_or));
    98	                } else if matches!(&q.body,Body::Scalar(Expr::Bool(b)) if *b==is_or) {
    99	                    let bad = Expr::Binary(
   100	                        Bin::Eq,
   101	                        Box::new(Expr::Assert(Box::new(Expr::Arg(
   102	                            "n".into(),
   103	                            Type::Nullable(Box::new(Type::Int)),
   104	                        )))),
   105	                        Box::new(Expr::Int(0)),
   106	                    );
   107	                    q.body = Body::Scalar(Expr::Binary(
   108	                        if is_or { Bin::Or } else { Bin::And },
   109	                        Box::new(Expr::Bool(is_or)),
   110	                        Box::new(bad),
   111	                    ));
   112	                } else {
   113	                    return None;
   114	                }
   115	            }
   116	        }
   117	        Some(q)
   118	    }
   119	}
   120	/// Whether an expression performs a read (which the binder hoists).
   121	fn reads(e: &Expr) -> bool {
   122	    match e {
   123	        Expr::Score(_) | Expr::Present(_) | Expr::Point(..) => true,
   124	        Expr::Binary(_, a, b) => reads(a) || reads(b),
   125	        Expr::Identity(a) | Expr::Assert(a) | Expr::Calendar(_, a) => reads(a),
   126	        _ => false,
   127	    }
   128	}

   201	#[derive(Default, Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
   202	pub struct Counts {

   204	    pub passed: u64,
   205	    pub unsupported: BTreeMap<String, u64>,
   206	    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "BTreeMap::is_empty")]
   207	    pub named: BTreeMap<String, u64>,

   209	    pub signatures: BTreeMap<String, u64>,
   210	    pub meter_fields_omitted: u64,
   211	}
   212	impl Counts {
   213	    pub fn record(&mut self, v: &Verdict) {
   214	        self.attempted += 1;
   215	        match v {
   216	            Verdict::Pass => self.passed += 1,
   217	            Verdict::Unsupported(s) => *self.unsupported.entry(s.clone()).or_default() += 1,
   218	            Verdict::Named(s) => *self.named.entry(s.clone()).or_default() += 1,
   219	            Verdict::Disagree(f) => {
   220	                self.disagreeing += 1;
   221	                *self.signatures.entry(f.signature.clone()).or_default() += 1;
   222	            }
   223	        }
   224	    }
   225	}
   226	fn class(o: &Outcome) -> String {
   227	    if let Some(r) = o.refusal() {
   228	        return r["code"].as_str().unwrap_or("refusal").to_owned();
   229	    }
   230	    match o.data() {
   231	        Some(Json::Object(v)) => v
   232	            .keys()
   233	            .next()
   234	            .map(String::as_str)
   235	            .unwrap_or("object")

   631	/-- The exact positive where witness of PAGE4. Other audiences cannot
   632	contribute to W; its readable source may certify the narrower page. -/
   633	def Expr.isWhereWitness (r : Rule) (e : Expr) : Bool :=
   634	  match r, e with
   635	  | .probe rt _ rkeys, .bin .ne (.point et ekeys) (.lit (.s .null)) =>
   636	      rt == et && rkeys.length == ekeys.length && rkeys.all fun rk =>
   637	        ekeys.any fun ek => rk.1 == ek.1 && match rk.2, ek.2 with
   638	          | .col .self c, .dot d => c == d
   639	          | .viewer, .viewer => true
   640	          | .lit a, .lit (.s b) => a == b
   641	          | _, _ => false
   642	  | _, _ => false

   644	def scopedWitnessDecided (sch : Schema) (td : TableDecl)
   645	    (pins : List (String × Expr)) (fs : List Expr) (order : List String) : Bool :=
   646	  td.read.disjuncts.any fun r => match r with
   647	    | .probe t point key =>
   648	        fs.any (Expr.isWhereWitness r) && (fs.filter (!·.isWhereWitness r)).all Expr.total

   650	          && (membershipColumns key).any fun pair =>
   651	            !pins.any (·.1 == pair.2)
   652	              && (!order.contains pair.2 || pair.2 == "id" && order == ["id"])

   654	    | _ => false

     4	/// The executable predicate classifier currently knows literal/viewer point
     5	/// terms only. Preserve an explicit boundary for admitted native argument
     6	/// and signed-expression keys rather than reporting a model disagreement
     7	/// under an incompletely translated completeness proof.
     8	pub(super) fn static_key_issue(read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<&'static str> {
     9	    fn missing(expr: &Expr) -> bool {
    10	        match expr {
    11	            Expr::Exists { read, .. } => read.keys.iter().all(|key| model_independent(&key.value))
    12	                && read.keys.iter().any(|key|
    13	                !matches!(&key.value, Expr::Literal { .. })
    14	                && !matches!(&key.value, Expr::Name { name, .. } if name == "viewer")),
    15	            Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => missing(left) || missing(right),
    16	            Expr::Unary { value, .. } => missing(value),
    17	            _ => false,
    18	        }
    19	    }
    20	    read.filter.as_deref().is_some_and(missing).then_some("page-static-key-expression")

    23	// @ref LLP 3000.036.001#16 — reproduce implicit bounds from surface
    24	// constraints, independently of the planner's selected index or IR.
    25	pub(super) fn implicit_page(module: &LeanModule, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<ReadExpr> {
    26	    if read.range.is_some() || !read.keys.is_empty() || read.index.is_some() { return None; }
    27	    let target = module.tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == read.table)?;
    28	    let mut predicates = Vec::new();
    29	    if let Some(filter) = &read.filter { flatten_and(filter, &mut predicates); }
    30	    let pinned: Vec<String> = predicates.iter().filter_map(|predicate| model_equality_column(target, predicate)).collect();
    31	    let cap = target.caps.iter().filter(|(_, by)| by.iter().all(|column| pinned.contains(column)
    32	        && !target.cols.iter().any(|col| &col.name == column && col.on_delete == Some("setNull"))))
    33	        .map(|(max, _)| *max).chain(target.uniques.iter().filter(|key| key.iter().all(|column|
    34	            pinned.contains(column) && (column == "id" || target.cols.iter().any(|col| &col.name == column && !col.nullable))))
    35	            .map(|_| 1)).min()?;
    36	    if cap > 8192 { return None; }
    37	    let mut page = read.clone();
    38	    page.range = Some(ast::RangeSpec { order: Order::Asc, take: cap, cursor: None, site: read.site.clone() });
    39	    Some(page)
    40	}

    42	pub(super) fn membership_physical_issue(module: &LeanModule, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<&'static str> {
    43	    if read.range.is_none() || !read.keys.is_empty() { return None }
    44	    let target = module.tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == read.table)?;
    45	    let decl = module.surface_tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == read.table)?;
    46	    let rule = decl.rules.iter().find(|rule| rule.effect == Effect::Read && rule.bindings.is_empty()).map(|rule| &rule.value)?;
    47	    let mut leaves = Vec::new();
    48	    flatten_or(rule, &mut leaves);
    49	    let mut parties = Vec::new();
    50	    let mut memberships = Vec::new();
    51	    for leaf in leaves {
    52	        if let Some(column) = party_column(target, leaf) { parties.push(column.to_owned()); }
    53	        else if let Some(membership) = membership_physical(module, target, leaf) { memberships.push(membership); }
    54	        else { return None; }
    55	    }
    56	    if memberships.is_empty() { return None }
    57	    if memberships.iter().any(|membership| !membership.source_admissible) {
    58	        return Some("page-membership-source-unique");
    59	    }
    60	    let mut fits = Vec::new();
    61	    for membership in &memberships {
    62	        let Some(table) = module.tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == membership.source) else { return Some("page-membership-key-bound") };
    63	        fits.push(match &membership.index {
    64	            Some(index) => model_selected_index_fit(module, table, index),
    65	            None => model_plain_index_fit(module, table, &membership.columns, false),
    66	        });
    67	    }
    68	    let (system_pins, branches) = model_page_pins(target, read);
    69	    fits.push(model_plain_index_fit(module, target, &model_lane_columns(system_pins, &read.order_keys), true));
    70	    for branch in branches {
    71	        for prefix in parties.iter().chain(memberships.iter().map(|membership| &membership.target_parent)) {
    72	            let mut pins = branch.clone();
    73	            if !pins.contains(prefix) { pins.push(prefix.clone()); pins.sort(); }
    74	            fits.push(model_plain_index_fit(module, target, &model_lane_columns(pins, &read.order_keys), true));
    75	        }
    76	    }
    77	    if fits.contains(&ModelIndexFit::Ambiguous) { Some("page-membership-index-selection") }
    78	    else if fits.contains(&ModelIndexFit::Nonportable) { Some("page-membership-key-bound") }
    79	    else { None }
    80	}

    85	pub(super) fn compound_physical_issue(module: &LeanModule, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<&'static str> {

   103	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "and" => {

   167	/// The model's direct grant theorem remains positive. Standard bounded
   168	/// negative-grant pages use the independent branch classifier; direct scalar
   169	/// absence/aggregation and fallible-filter fallback stay explicitly outside
   170	/// that executable completeness fragment until its negative-view proof lands.
   171	pub(super) fn negative_grant_issue(module: &LeanModule, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<&'static str> {
   172	    if !read.keys.is_empty() && read.filter.is_none() && read.range.is_none() { return None; }
   173	    fn negative_probe(expr: &Expr) -> bool {
   174	        match expr {
   175	            Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } if op=="not" => matches!(value.as_ref(),Expr::Exists {..}),
   176	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op=="and" || op=="or" => negative_probe(left) || negative_probe(right),
   177	            _=>false,
   178	        }
   179	    }
   180	    let table = module.surface_tables.iter().find(|table| table.name == read.table)?;
   181	    let rule = &table.rules.iter().find(|rule| rule.effect == Effect::Read)?.value;
   182	    if !negative_probe(rule) { return None; }
   183	    fn total(expr: &Expr) -> bool {
   184	        match expr {
   185	            Expr::Name { .. } | Expr::Literal { .. } | Expr::Focal { .. } => true,
   186	            Expr::Member { base, .. } => matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Focal { .. }),
   187	            Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } => matches!(op.as_str(), "=" | "!=" | "<" | "<=" | ">" | ">=" | "and" | "or" | "in") && total(left) && total(right),
   188	            Expr::Unary { op, value, .. } => op == "not" && total(value),
   189	            Expr::Array { items, .. } => items.iter().all(total), _ => false,
   190	        }

  1228	fn model_page_pins(table: &Table, read: &ReadExpr) -> (Vec<String>, Vec<Vec<String>>) {
  1229	    let Some(filter) = read.filter.as_deref() else { return (Vec::new(), vec![Vec::new()]) };

  1236	    flatten_or(expression, &mut alternatives);
  1237	    let rest: Vec<_> = conjuncts.iter().enumerate().filter_map(|(at, expression)| (at != *position).then_some(*expression)).collect();

  1239	        let mut parts = Vec::new();
  1240	        flatten_and(alternative, &mut parts);
  1241	        let alternative_pins = model_pin_columns(table, &parts, &read.order_keys);
  1242	        if alternative_pins.is_empty() { return None }
  1243	        parts.extend(rest.iter().copied());
  1244	        Some(model_pin_columns(table, &parts, &read.order_keys))
  1245	    }).collect();

   951	      -- top-N answer has no `next`" (LLP 3000.027 §1; OPEN C9).
   952	      pure (v, if after.isSome then nx else some .null)
   953	    | .count t pins f => do
   954	      let ps ← evPins pins
   955	      countRead cx vw t ps (filt f)
   956	    | .exists_ t pins f => do
   957	      let ps ← evPins pins
   958	      -- `exists` over branches is an `or` of decided existence reads, for
   959	      -- parties only (LLP 3000.036.001 §8); its pins' form decides it too.

   437	pub fn repeat(case: &Case, source: &str) -> Result<(Verdict, u64), String> {
   438	    let (mut left, p) = execute::world(case, source)?;
   439	    let (mut right, q) = execute::world(case, source)?;
   440	    if let Some(v) = prelude_disagreement(&Oracle::Repeat, &p, &q, false) {
   441	        return Ok((v, 2 * p.len() as u64));
   442	    }
   443	    let a = execute::execute(&mut left, case, "run", &case.args);
   444	    let b = execute::execute(&mut right, case, "run", &case.args);
   445	    if let Some(f) = field(&a, &b, false) {
   446	        return Ok((disagreement(&Oracle::Repeat, &f, a, b, vec![]), 2));
   447	    }
   448	    if case.program.mutation() {
   449	        let x = execute::execute(&mut left, case, "observe", &case.args);
   450	        let y = execute::execute(&mut right, case, "observe", &case.args);
   451	        if let Some(f) = field(&x, &y, false) {
   452	            return Ok((
   453	                disagreement(&Oracle::Repeat, &format!("post-{f}"), x, y, vec![a, b]),
   454	                4,
   455	            ));
   456	        }
   457	        return Ok((Verdict::Pass, 4));
   458	    }
   459	    Ok((Verdict::Pass, 2))
   460	}

    30	fn body(statements: &mut Vec<Stmt>) {
    31	    for stmt in statements.iter_mut() {
    32	        match stmt {
    33	            Stmt::If { then_body, else_body, .. } => { body(then_body); body(else_body); }
    34	            Stmt::Return { value, site } => {
    35	                if let Some(lowered) = page(value, site) { *stmt = lowered; }
    36	            }
    37	            _ => {}
    38	        }
    39	    }
    40	}

    42	/// The returned read, through one projection.
    43	fn read_of(value: &mut Expr) -> Option<&mut ReadExpr> {
    44	    match value {
    45	        Expr::Read(read) => Some(read),
    46	        Expr::ShapeUse { base, .. } | Expr::Shape { base, .. } => match base.as_mut() { Expr::Read(read) => Some(read), _ => None },
    47	        _ => None,
    48	    }
    49	}
    50	
    51	pub(crate) struct Split { guard: Expr, conjunctive: bool, rest: Option<Expr> }
    52	
    53	fn page(value: &Expr, site: &crate::diag::Site) -> Option<Stmt> {
    54	    let mut value = value.clone();
    55	    let read = read_of(&mut value)?;
    56	    let Split { guard, conjunctive, rest } = split(read.filter.as_deref()?)?;
    57	    let empty = Expr::Array { items: Vec::new(), site: site.clone() };
    58	    let with = |filter: Option<Expr>| { let mut value = value.clone(); read_of(&mut value).unwrap().filter = filter.map(Box::new); value };
    59	    let (then, otherwise) = if conjunctive {
    60	        // `G and R`: G holds → the page over R; otherwise no row passes.
    61	        (with(rest), empty)
    62	    } else {
    63	        // `G or R`: G holds → every row passes; otherwise the page over R.
    64	        let otherwise = if rest.is_some() { with(rest) } else { empty };
    65	        (with(None), otherwise)
    66	    };
    67	    Some(Stmt::If { condition: guard, then_body: vec![Stmt::Return { value: then, site: site.clone() }], else_body: vec![Stmt::Return { value: otherwise, site: site.clone() }], site: site.clone() })
    68	}
    69	
    70	/// The guard of a page filter, when one can move out of the scan.
    71	pub(crate) fn split(filter: &Expr) -> Option<Split> {
    72	    let conjunctive = matches!(filter, Expr::Binary { op, .. } if op == "and");
    73	    let op = if conjunctive { "and" } else { "or" };
    74	    let mut parts = Vec::new();
    75	    chain(filter, op, &mut parts);
    76	    let last = parts.iter().rposition(|part| guard(part))?;
    77	    // Every operand evaluated per row before the last guard must be unable to refuse.
    78	    if !parts[..last].iter().all(|part| pure(part)) { return None; }
    79	    let (guards, rest): (Vec<Expr>, Vec<Expr>) = parts.into_iter().partition(guard);
    80	    let join = |items: Vec<Expr>| items.into_iter().reduce(|left, right| Expr::Binary { site: left.site().clone(), op: op.into(), left: Box::new(left), right: Box::new(right) });
    81	    Some(Split { guard: join(guards)?, conjunctive, rest: join(rest) })
    82	}
    83	
    84	/// Whether a page filter holds a guard this lowering moves.
    85	pub(crate) fn has_guard(filter: &Expr) -> bool { split(filter).is_some() }

    87	fn chain(value: &Expr, op: &str, out: &mut Vec<Expr>) {
    88	    if let Expr::Binary { op: here, left, right, .. } = value && here == op { chain(left, op, out); chain(right, op, out); }
    89	    else { out.push(value.clone()); }
    90	}

    98	        Expr::Member { base, .. } | Expr::Unary { value: base, .. } => focal(base),

   101	        _ => false,
   102	    }
   103	}

   107	fn pure(value: &Expr) -> bool {

   278	/// How often each name is bound or assigned anywhere in `body` (an argument
   279	/// that appears here no longer holds the value the scan's prefix was
   280	/// evaluated from; a short-circuit temporary is assigned exactly twice).
   281	fn assignment_counts(body: &[crate::ir::Stmt], out: &mut BTreeMap<String, usize>) {
   282	    use crate::ir::Stmt;
   283	    for statement in body {
   284	        match statement {
   285	            Stmt::Let { name, .. } | Stmt::Const { name, .. } | Stmt::Assign { name, .. } => *out.entry(name.clone()).or_default() += 1,
   286	            Stmt::If { then_body, else_body, .. } => { assignment_counts(then_body, out); assignment_counts(else_body, out); }
   287	            Stmt::For { binding, body, .. } => { *out.entry(binding.clone()).or_default() += 1; assignment_counts(body, out); }
   288	            _ => {}
   289	        }
   290	    }
   291	}
   292	
   293	/// The compiler lowers a short-circuit `a and b` (`a or b`) to a temporary:
   294	/// `$bool1 = a`, then `if $bool1: $bool1 = b` (`if not $bool1: …`, or
   295	/// `if $bool1:` with the assignment in its `else`). Each such
   296	/// temporary, assigned only there, stands for the whole condition.
   297	fn short_circuit_bindings(body: &[crate::ir::Stmt]) -> BTreeMap<String, Expr> {
   298	    use crate::ir::{BinaryOp, Stmt, UnaryOp};
   299	    fn collect(body: &[Stmt], counts: &BTreeMap<String, usize>, out: &mut BTreeMap<String, Expr>) {
   300	        for pair in body.windows(2) {
   301	            let [Stmt::Let { name, value: first }, Stmt::If { condition, then_body, else_body }] = pair else { continue };
   302	            if !name.starts_with('$') || counts.get(name) != Some(&2) { continue; }
   303	            // `if $b: $b = second` (and), `if not $b: $b = second` or
   304	            // `if $b: (nothing) else: $b = second` (or).
   305	            let tested = |negated: bool| match condition {
   306	                Expr::Variable(tested) => !negated && tested == name,
   307	                Expr::Unary { op: UnaryOp::Not, value } => negated && matches!(value.as_ref(), Expr::Variable(tested) if tested == name),
   308	                _ => false,
   309	            };
   310	            let (op, body) = if else_body.is_empty() && tested(false) { (BinaryOp::And, then_body) }
   311	                else if else_body.is_empty() && tested(true) { (BinaryOp::Or, then_body) }
   312	                else if then_body.is_empty() && tested(false) { (BinaryOp::Or, else_body) }
   313	                else { continue };
   314	            // The second operand's own setup (a probe's `$probe2 = t[key]`) precedes the assignment.
   315	            let Some((Stmt::Assign { name: assigned, value: second }, setup)) = body.split_last() else { continue };
   316	            if !setup.iter().all(|statement| matches!(statement, Stmt::Let { .. } | Stmt::Const { .. })) { continue; }
   317	            if assigned != name { continue; }
   318	            out.insert(name.clone(), Expr::Binary { op, left: Box::new(first.clone()), right: Box::new(second.clone()) });
   319	        }
   320	        for statement in body {
   321	            match statement {
   322	                Stmt::If { then_body, else_body, .. } => { collect(then_body, counts, out); collect(else_body, counts, out); }
   323	                Stmt::For { body, .. } => collect(body, counts, out),
   324	                _ => {}
   325	            }
   326	        }
   327	    }
   328	    let mut counts = BTreeMap::new();
   329	    assignment_counts(body, &mut counts);
   330	    let mut out = BTreeMap::new();
   331	    collect(body, &counts, &mut out);
   332	    out
   333	}

   335	struct Guard<'a> {
   336	    grant: &'a RuleExpr,
   337	    written: &'a BTreeSet<String>,
   338	    probes: &'a BTreeMap<String, crate::ir::Read>,
   339	    conditions: &'a BTreeMap<String, Expr>,
   340	    /// The scan's pinned columns whose values cannot change before the walk.
   341	    pins: &'a [(&'a String, &'a Expr)],
   342	}
   343	
   344	/// One fact a condition establishes: `left op right` with `op` `=`/`!=`, or
   345	/// an `exists` probe that holds.
   346	#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
   347	enum Fact<'a> { Exists(&'a crate::ir::Read), Compare(bool, &'a Expr, &'a Expr) }
   348	
   349	impl Guard<'_> {
   350	    /// The alternatives `condition` being true establishes, each a set of
   351	    /// facts that hold together (disjunctive normal form): `a or b` is two
   352	    /// alternatives, `a and b` one with the facts of both. A part we cannot
   353	    /// name contributes no fact, so an alternative built on it proves nothing.
   354	    // @ref LLP 3000.036#14.1 — a guard `if exists A or exists B:` holds a grant on each alternative.
   355	    fn when_true<'e>(&'e self, condition: &'e Expr) -> Vec<Vec<Fact<'e>>> {
   356	        use crate::ir::{BinaryOp, UnaryOp};
   357	        match condition {
   358	            Expr::Variable(name) if self.conditions.contains_key(name) => self.when_true(&self.conditions[name]),
   359	            Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::And, left, right } => product(self.when_true(left), self.when_true(right)),
   360	            Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Or, left, right } => union(self.when_true(left), self.when_true(right)),
   361	            Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Eq, left, right } => vec![vec![Fact::Compare(true, left, right)]],
   362	            Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Ne, left, right } => vec![vec![Fact::Compare(false, left, right)]],
   363	            Expr::Unary { op: UnaryOp::Not, value } => self.when_false(value),
   364	            Expr::Exists(read) => vec![vec![Fact::Exists(read)]],
   365	            _ => vec![Vec::new()],
   366	        }
   367	    }
   368	
   369	    /// The alternatives `condition` being false establishes.
   370	    fn when_false<'e>(&'e self, condition: &'e Expr) -> Vec<Vec<Fact<'e>>> {
   371	        use crate::ir::{BinaryOp, UnaryOp};
   372	        match condition {
   373	            Expr::Variable(name) if self.conditions.contains_key(name) => self.when_false(&self.conditions[name]),
   374	            Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Or, left, right } => product(self.when_false(left), self.when_false(right)),
   375	            Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::And, left, right } => union(self.when_false(left), self.when_false(right)),
   376	            Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Eq, left, right } => vec![vec![Fact::Compare(false, left, right)]],
   377	            Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Ne, left, right } => vec![vec![Fact::Compare(true, left, right)]],
   378	            Expr::Unary { op: UnaryOp::Not, value } => self.when_true(value),
   379	            _ => vec![Vec::new()],
   380	        }
   381	    }

    69	/// Give every generated access row a required item reference, add the total
    70	/// `(item, principal)` membership point and ensure the case viewer holds one.
    71	/// PAGE2 then discovers target ids through `(principal, item)` and opens a
    72	/// fully pinned physical byId lane for each target.
    73	pub(crate) fn id_membership(case: &mut Case) {
    74	    let items = case.rows.iter().filter(|(table, _)| table == "items")
    75	        .filter_map(|(_, row)| row["id"].as_str().map(str::to_owned)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    76	    let access = case.program.tables.iter_mut().find(|table| table.name == "access").expect("generated access table");
    77	    access.columns.push(column("item", Type::Reference("items".into())));
    78	    // The competing `(bucket, principal)` unique proves that every positional
    79	    // planner resolves intrinsic `.id` by its focal-table reference type.
    80	    access.keys.push(("byItem".into(), vec!["item".into(), "principal".into()], true));
    81	    access.keys.push(("byPrincipalItem".into(), vec!["principal".into(), "item".into()], false));
    82	    if items.is_empty() {
    83	        case.rows.retain(|(table, _)| table != "access");
    84	        return;
    85	    }
    86	    let mut viewer = false;
    87	    let mut n = 0;
    88	    for (table, row) in &mut case.rows {
    89	        if table != "access" { continue; }
    90	        row.as_object_mut().expect("generated access row").insert("item".into(), json!(items[n % items.len()]));
    91	        viewer |= row["principal"] == case.principal;
    92	        n += 1;
    93	    }
    94	    let mut pairs = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
    95	    case.rows.retain(|(table, row)| table != "access" || pairs.insert((row["item"].to_string(), row["principal"].to_string())));
    96	    if !viewer {
    97	        case.rows.push(("access".into(), json!({"id":format!("page-id-{}", case.seed),
    98	            "bucket":0,"principal":case.principal,"item":items[n % items.len()]})));
    99	    }
   100	}
   101	
   102	/// Make an uncapped positional membership point nullable while a separate
   103	/// fully fixed total unique bounds its inverted discovery to one source row.
   104	pub(crate) fn discovery_unique_membership(case: &mut Case) {
   105	    let items = &mut case.program.tables[0];
   106	    items.effects = Some(vec![Rule::OwnerOrWitness, Rule::Allow, Rule::Allow, Rule::Allow]);
   107	    let access = case.program.tables.iter_mut().find(|table| table.name == "access")
   108	        .expect("generated access table");
   109	    access.columns.iter_mut().find(|column| column.name == "bucket")
   110	        .expect("generated membership parent").ty = Type::Nullable(Box::new(Type::Int));
   111	    access.keys.push(("byPrincipalOnly".into(), vec!["principal".into()], true));
   112	    access.rule = Rule::Member;
   113	    access.effects = Some(vec![Rule::Member, Rule::Allow, Rule::Allow, Rule::Allow]);
   114	    access.residency = Residency::Online;
   115	    let bucket = case.args["bucket"].as_u64().expect("generated bucket");
   116	    let viewer = case.principal.clone();
   117	    let other = if viewer == "alice" { "bob" } else { "alice" };
   118	    case.rows.retain(|(table, _)| table != "access");
   119	    case.rows.push(("access".into(), json!({"id":"discovery-viewer", "bucket":bucket, "principal":viewer})));
   120	    case.rows.push(("access".into(), json!({"id":"discovery-other", "bucket":1 - bucket, "principal":other})));
   121	    case.program.membership_named_reordered = false;
   122	}

  1012	        RuleExpr::Allow | RuleExpr::Public { .. } => truth,

  1014	        RuleExpr::Eq(left, right) => pinned_boolean_equality(left, right, pinned).is_some_and(|equal| equal == truth)

  1019	        RuleExpr::Ne(left, right) => pinned_boolean_equality(left, right, pinned).is_some_and(|equal| equal != truth),

  1021	        RuleExpr::Or(rules) => rules.iter().all(|rule| rule_known(rule, false, table_name, table, pinned)),
  1022	        RuleExpr::And(rules) if truth => !rules.is_empty() && rules.iter().all(|rule| rule_known(rule, true, table_name, table, pinned)),

+  return items first 10 by .at
+");
+    assert!(!product.schema.required_capabilities().contains("branch-pages-v4"));
+    let mut engine = Engine::memory(product.schema, product.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
+    engine.seed([
+        ("items".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("a")), ("owner", id("alice")), ("helper", id("bob")), ("status", Value::String("open".into())), ("at", Value::Int(1))])),
+        ("items".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("b")), ("owner", id("bob")), ("helper", id("alice")), ("status", Value::String("active".into())), ("at", Value::Int(2))])),
+        ("items".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("c")), ("owner", id("alice")), ("helper", id("bob")), ("status", Value::String("closed".into())), ("at", Value::Int(3))])),
+    ]).unwrap();
+    let result = engine.query("page", context("alice", &[])).unwrap();
+    assert!(result.complete && result.next.is_none(), "{result:?}");
+    let ids: Vec<_> = result.data.as_array().unwrap().iter().map(|row| row.field("id").unwrap().clone()).collect();
+    assert_eq!(ids, [id("a"), id("b")]);
+
+    let fixed = bound("table chapters:
+  bucket: int
+  principal: principal
+  unique bucket, principal
+  read <- .principal = viewer
+  online only
+table access:
+  item: items
+  principal: principal
+  unique item, principal
+  read <- .principal = viewer
+  online only
+table items:
+  bucket: int
+  at: int
+  read <- exists chapters[.bucket, viewer] and exists access[.id, viewer]
+  online only
+query page(bucket: int, c: cursor ?):
+  return items where .bucket = bucket first 1 by .at after c
+");
+    assert!(fixed.schema.required_capabilities().contains("branch-pages-v4"));
+    let mut engine = Engine::memory(fixed.schema, fixed.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
+    engine.seed([
+        ("chapters".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("chapter")), ("bucket", Value::Int(1)), ("principal", id("alice"))])),
+        ("access".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("access")), ("item", id("visible")), ("principal", id("alice"))])),
+        ("items".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("visible")), ("bucket", Value::Int(1)), ("at", Value::Int(1))])),
+        ("items".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("hidden")), ("bucket", Value::Int(1)), ("at", Value::Int(2))])),
+    ]).unwrap();
+    let result = engine.query("page", context("alice", &[("bucket", Value::Int(1)), ("c", Value::Null)])).unwrap();
+    assert!(result.complete && result.next.is_none(), "{result:?}");
+    assert_eq!(result.data.as_array().unwrap()[0].field("id").unwrap(), &id("visible"));
+
+    let vacancy = bound("table holds:
+  item: designs
+  owner: principal
+  unique item
+  read <- .owner = viewer
+  online only
+table designs:
+  owner: principal
+  read <- not exists holds[item = .id] or .owner = viewer
+  online only
+query available(c: cursor ?):
+  return designs first 1 by .id after c
+");
+    assert!(vacancy.schema.required_capabilities().contains("branch-pages-v4"));
+    let mut engine = Engine::memory(vacancy.schema, vacancy.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
+    engine.seed([
+        ("holds".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("hold")), ("item", id("held")), ("owner", id("alice"))])),
+        ("designs".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("free")), ("owner", id("alice"))])),
+        ("designs".into(), snapback4_core::value::object([("id", id("held")), ("owner", id("alice"))])),
+    ]).unwrap();
+    let result = engine.query("available", context("bob", &[("c", Value::Null)])).unwrap();
+    assert!(result.complete && result.next.is_none(), "{result:?}");
+    assert_eq!(result.data.as_array().unwrap()[0].field("id").unwrap(), &id("free"));
+}
